# The Gadl of Abba Fiqəṭor (Il Gädl di)

**Author**: Anonymous  
**Series**: PO Tome 51  
**Columns**: pp. 162–244  

[p. 162] {#po.51.p162}
በስመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ አብ ፣ ቀዳማዊ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጥንት ፣ ወዳ ኃራዊ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ምፍጻም ፡ ፈጣሬ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ስም ፡ ዘይሰመይ ፡፣ ዐጻዌዊ ፣ ቀላያት ፣ ግሩም ፡ ዘይጐልቆ ፡ ለነጠብጣበ ፡፣ ዝናም ፡ ወያበልዮ ፡ ለአክራም ። መንበረ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ፡ አርያም ፡ ወቤተ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፣ ኤዶም ፡ ወመካነ ፡ መለ ኮቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፣ ዓለም ። አልቦ ፡ ወሰን' ፡ ወኢዐቅመ ፡ እስከ ፡ በርባ ሮስ ፣ ይፀብት ፡፣ ወይትዐደው ፡ መትሕተ ፣ ታሕቲት ። እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ መልዕ ልተ ፡ ሰማያት ፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ለዓለም ።
1 In the name of God the Father, the first without beginning and the last without end, creator of all that has a name, the fearsome one who closes the abysses (Odes 12:3), he who counts the drops of rain and makes the years flow. Exalted is the throne of his glory, Edom is the house of his grace, and the dwelling of his power is in the whole world (Ps 103:22). There is no boundary nor measure, he swims to the barbaros and surpasses the most extreme depths. While standing above the heavens, he is the God blessed for the world (Rom 9:5).

### The Son
ወበስመ ፣ ወልዱ ፣ ዋሕድ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ዘተወልደ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ በአካል ፣ ወበአምሳል ፡ ዘኢቀደሞ ፡ ዓመታት ፡ ወአዝማን ። ኢሰዐት፡ ፡ ወኢቅ
2 And in the name of his only Son Jesus Christ who was born of him in person and in image (Heb 1:3), whom neither the years nor the epochs preceded.
ጽበተ ፣ ዐይን ። ወኢይትበሀል ፣ ዛጊዜ ፣ ተወልደ ፣ ወበዘከመዝ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ ወለዶ ። አልቦ ፣ ሰዓት፤ ፣ ወአልቦ ፣ መዋዕል‹ ፣ አመ ፣ ወለዶ ። ኢተባሕ ተወ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ቦቱ ፣ ወኢይትአመር ፡ ልደቱ ፡ እምአብ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዕፁብ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ወኢይትዐወቅ ፣ ጥንተ ፣ ህላዌሁ ፣ እስመ ፣ ሥውር ፣ ውእቱ ። ወኢ ይጤይቆ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወዘመላእክት ፡ አእምሮ ፣ ወኢመኑሂ ፡ ኢይክል ፡ ኀልዮቶ' ፡ እመኒ ፣ ዐርገ ፣ ሰማየ ፣ ወእመኒ ፡ ወረደ ፣ ምድረ ፣ ወኀለፈ ፣ ባሕረ ፡ ወቀላየ ፡፣ እሳት ፡ ወነፋስ ። ወለእመ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምህየ ፣ ወኀሠሠ ፡ ይምነ ፡ ወፅግመ ፣ ኢበጸቢተ ፣ ሕሊና ፡ ወኢበምክር ፣ ኢይክል ፡ ያእምር ፣ መጠነ ፣ መዋዕሊሁ ። ዘውእቱ ፡ ዋሕድ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ወዕሩይ ፡፣ ምሰለ ፣ ወላዲሁ ። ብርሃን ፣ ዘእምብርሃን ፡ አምላክ ፣ ዘእምአምላክ ፣ ዘበአማን ፡ ቀዳማዊ ፡ ወዳኃራዊ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ ነጐድጓድ ፡ አነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀዳማዊ ፡ ወአነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዳኃራዊ ፣ ርእስ ፡ ወማሕለቅት ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፣ መጽሐፈ ፡ ሕይወት ። ቀዳሚሁ ፡ ቃል ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡፣ ውእቱ ፣ ቃል ። ወከማሁ ፣ ቀዳ ሚሁ ፣ እምቀዲሙ ፡፣ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ውእቱ፤ ወኩሉ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ኮነ ፡
Neither the hour, nor the blink of an eye. ΓΕ it is not said: "He was born at that time and in such days he begot him." There is no hour and there are no days in which he begot him. His Father was (never) alone; his birth from the Father is not known because it is difficult, and his primordial existence is not known because it is hidden (Rom 16:25). The mind of man does not comprehend it, nor the knowledge of the angels; and no one manages to conceive it, [not even if he goes up to heaven, and not even if he descends to earth, nor if he crosses the sea and the depth of fire and wind. And even if he were to go out from here and search to the right and left, neither with the swimming of the thought nor with counsel can he know the quantity of his days. This is the Only-Begotten for his Father and co-equal with his Begetter. 3 Light from light, truly God from God, the first and the last. As he himself said to the son of Thunder: "I am the first, I am the last, the beginning and the end" (Rev 1:8, 17). And again the book of life says: "In the beginning was the Word, and that Word was with God, and that Word was God. And thus the primordial (Word) was with God from the beginning, and everything was made in him, and without him nothing was made, and absolutely nothing" (Jn 1:1-3).
ወዘእንበሌሁሰ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡፣ ወኢምንተኒ ። ኖሁ ፣ ተዐውቀ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ምስለ ፣ አቡሁ ። እምቅድመ ፡ ግብረተ ፣ መላእክት ፡ ወሰብእ ፣ ወእምቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይትነፋሕ ፡ ኃይለ ፣ ነፋሳት ፣ ወያስተርኢ ፡ ሐውዘ ፣ አየራት ። ወዘእንበለ ፡ ይትመላጥ ፣ መባርቅት ። እምቅድመ ፣ ይድምፅ ፣ ነጐድጓድ ፡ ወይስፋሕ ፡ ደመናት ፡፣ ወለዶ ፡፣ አብ ፣ ለወልዱ ፡ ወአልቦ ፣ አመ ፡ ኢወለዶ ። ውእቱ ፡ ኀይሉ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ጥበቡ ፡ ወምክሩ ፡ ወክቡር ፡ ዘከማሁ ፣ ዘሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጠር ፣ ዓለም ። ዝኬ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ዘተወልደ ፡ በደኃሪ ፡ መዋ ዕል ፣ እማርያም ፣ እምቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ እንበለ ፡ አብ ፣ ምድራዊ ።
4 Behold, the existence of the Son with his Father has been known. Before the creation of the angels and of men, and before every creature, before the force of the winds blew and the sweetness of the air manifested itself. Before the lightnings were unsheathed. Before the thunder emitted sounds and the clouds were stretched out, the Father begot his Son, and there is not a moment in which he did not beget him. He is "the power of his Father, his wisdom" (1 Cor 1:24), his counsel and glorious like him, who was together with him before the world was created. It is precisely this one who in later days was born of Mary, of the holy virgin, without an earthly father.

አንደየ ፣ ርእሶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብዕለነ ፡ በትሕትናሁ ። ኮነ ፡ ሕፃነ ፡ ጠበወ ፡ ሐሊበ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሴስየነ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሞ" ፡ ለሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ሰከበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጎል ፡ ወተአስረ ፣ መንኮቢያቲሁ ፡ ከመ ፣ ያንሥእ ፣ ኪያነ ፡ ውዱ ቃነ ፣ ወይፍታሕ ፡ እምኔነ ፡ መአስረ ፡ ኃጣውኢነ ። ተጠብለለ ፡ በአፅርቅት" ፡ ከመ ፣ ያልብሰነ ፣ ግርማ ፣ መለኮቱ ። አንሶሰወ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወአስተር አየ ፣ ከመ ፣ ሰብእ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይረስየነ ፣ ወራስያነ ፣ መንግሥቱ ። ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፣ እምኅበ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ ከመ ፡ ያንጽ ሐነ ፣ እምርስሐት ። ዝውእቱ ፣ ዘልዑል ፡ እምሰማያት ፣ ወንጹሕ ፣ እምኀጢ አት ። ወሶበ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምጥማቃት ፡ ስምዐ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አብ ፡ በእን ቲአሁ ፣ እንዘ ፡፣ ይብል ፣ እምሰማይ ፣ ዝውእቱ ፣ ወልድየ ፣ ዘአፈቅር ፡ ወሎቱ ፣፡ ስምፅዎ ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ ንሕነ ፡ አመነ ፣ ወአእመርነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምስለ ፣ አብ ፡ ወአብ ፡ ቦቱ ። በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ለሊሁ ፣ በውስተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡
5 He made himself poor to make us rich with his humility (2 Cor 8:9). He became a child, he sucked milk, to nourish us with his body and with his blood for eternal life. He lay in a stable and his thumbs were tied so that he might lift us who had fallen, and untie from us the bonds of our sins. He was wrapped in swaddling clothes so that he might clothe us with the magnificence of his divinity. He moved on earth and manifested himself as man to make us heirs of his kingdom. He entered the Jordan and asked for baptism from John (Mt 3:13ff) to purify us from filth. This is he who is more exalted than the heavens and immune from sin. And when he descended into the lustral localities, the Father was witness to him when he said: "This is my Son whom I love, and listen to him!" (Mt 17:5). And for this we have believed and have known that he is with the Father and the Father in him (Jn 14:11; 17:21), as he himself says in the gospel: "I and the Father are one" (Jn 10:30).
አነ ፡ ወአብ ፡ ፳ ፡ ንሕነ ። ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቃና ፡ ዘገሊላ ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ ለማይ ፣ ወይነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ለነ ፣ ያስትየነ ፡ ወይነ ፣ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ወሐሜት ። ሰአለ ፣ ማየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጽሙእ ፡ በኅበ ፣ ብእሲት ፡ ሳምራዊት ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀበነ ፡ ፈልፈለ ፡ ማየ ፡ ሕይወት ፣ ዘውሕዘ ፣ እምገቦሁ ። ወነሥአ ፡ ድ ፣ ኅብስተ ፡ በገዳም ፡ ወአጽ ገበ ፡ ፪፻ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፣ አንስት ፣ ወደቅ ። ጸምአ ፡ ወርኅበ ፡ ወተመከረ ፡ እም ኀበ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ከመ ፣ ያግዕዘነ ፡ እምግብርናተ ፣ ኀጢአት ። ወዐርገ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ተጽዒኖ ፡ ፅዋለ ፣ አድግ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርእየነ ፣ ብዕለ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ። ሐፀበ ፡ እግረ ፡፣ አርዳኢሁ ፣ ቀኒቶ ፣ መክፌ ፡ ለንጽ ፣ ከመ ፡፣ ንትሉ ፡ ንሕነ ፣ አሰረ ፣ ዚአሁ ። ሐመ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያሕይወነ ፡ በሞቱ ። ወተንሥአ ፣ እሙታን ፣ በሣልስት ፡ ዕለት ፣ ከመ ፣ ያንሥአነ ፡ በትንሣኤሁ ።
6 And he went to Cana of Galilee and turned water into wine (Jn 2:1-12) to make us drink the wine of joy and gladness (Ps 104:15). He asked for water like a thirsty man from the Samaritan woman (Jn 4:7-42) to give us the spring of the water of life that gushed from his side (Jn 19:34). And he took five loaves in the desert and fed five thousand (people) without (counting) the women and children (Jn 6:1-13). He suffered thirst and hunger and was tempted by the devil to free us from the slavery of sin (Mt 4:1-11//). And he went up to Jerusalem riding a donkey to show us the wealth of his glory (Mt 21:1-11//). He washed the feet of his disciples, girding himself with a towel so that we (too) might follow in his footsteps (Jn 13:2-17). He suffered, died, and was buried (1 Cor 15:3-4) to vivify us with his death. He rose from death on the third day to make us rise through his resurrection.

7 He ascended to the heavens with glory and sat at the right hand of his Father, in the seat that he had (never) left; to his greatness belongs power.

8 And in the name of the Holy Spirit Paraclete, spirit of truth (Jn 14:17), spirit of faith, spirit of wisdom and of counsel, spirit of grace and of power (Is 11:2), co-equal with the Father and the Son. He is neither inferior (to the Father and the Son), nor superior. 9 Everything was created with his grace and everything subsists by his goodness. The Son is the Word of the Father and the Holy Spirit is the Spirit of both, the lord of knowledge, the fountainhead of wisdom that proceeds from the Father (Jn 14:16, 26) and that takes from the Son (Jn 16:7, 14). As our Savior himself bore witness concerning the Holy Spirit, saying: "And when that Spirit of truth comes, he will guide you into all truth, for he will not speak from himself, but what he has heard. He will tell you and will announce to you what will happen, and he will praise me and will glorify me because he will take from me and will tell it to you, and will announce everything to you. Everything that my Father possesses is mine. For this reason I told you that he will take from mine and will tell it to you and will announce it to you" (Jn 16:13-15).
ዝኬ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ መንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ወመንፈሰ ፡ ጽድቅ ። ወመጺኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፣ መንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ወመንፈሰ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡፣ በከመ ፣ ተናገረ ፡ መድ ኀኒነ ፡ እንዘ ፡፣ ይብል ፣ ወሶበ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ አነ ፣ እፌንዎ ፣ ለክሙ ። ወመ ጺኦ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ይዛለፎ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ በእንተ ፣ ኀጢአት ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ኩነኔ ። ዘንተሰ ፡ ዘይቤ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ኀጢአትሰ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢአምኑ ፣ ብየ ። ወበእንተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ እስመ ፡ አሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ አብ ። ወበእንተ ፣ ኩነኔ ፣ እስመ ፣ ይትኩነን ፣ መልአኩ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ። ወብየ ፡ ብዙኅ'፤ ፣ ነገር"፤ ፣ ዘእነግረክሙ ፣ ወባሕቱ ፣ ኢትክሉ ፡ ጸዊሮቶ ፡ ይእዜ ። አጠይቅኒኬ ፣ ኦመድኅን ፣ ዘለዘክሮተ ፡ ስምከ ፡፣ ይደሉ" ፡ ሰጊድ ፡ እስመ ፡
10 This is therefore the Holy Spirit, the Spirit of truth. And that Holy Spirit and the Spirit of truth came as our Savior had announced, saying: "And when I have gone, I will send him to you, and when he comes, he will rebuke the world regarding sin and justice and judgment" (Jn 16:8). And he said this, "regarding sin, because they have not believed in me; regarding justice, because I go to the Father. Regarding judgment, because the prince of this world will be condemned. And I have many things to tell you, but now you cannot bear them" (Jn 16:9-12). Convince me then, O Savior! to whose name (belongs) the bow, since you say: "Now you cannot bear them" (Jn 16:12).

11 You have said this to send them the power, as the Gospel of Luke says: "And behold, I send the promise of my Father upon you. But stay in the city of Jerusalem until you are clothed with power from on high" (Lk 24:49). This is the Spirit that makes one wise and the Spirit of instruction and power (2Tm 1:7) which descended upon the apostles in the upper room of Zion on the fiftieth day (Acts 2:1-3). rock (Mt 16:18) of the faith: "This office is not from us, but our Lord promised us that He would send us the Paraclete, who is the Holy Spirit, and He will make us understand everything that we must proclaim and do." And while Peter was saying this word to them, behold, they heard a sound like the sound of a whirlwind. And they smelled a sweet fragrance that they had never known before in the world. And tongues of fire appeared in their midst, descending upon each of them (Acts 2:1-3). And they began to speak a new language wherever they went to call people to the faith. And He indicated to them what they had to do, so that they might teach people prayer, service, the law, and the order. And they thanked God for having instructed them. For this reason, therefore, we have known that He had reproved the world.

12 As for what He had said to them: "Now you cannot bear it" (Jn 16:12), through this we have therefore understood, when His Spirit and the Spirit of His Father descended upon them on the fiftieth day: they were able to bear it. This Holy Trinity is one God. A beginning without an "at that time," and an origin without a "where," and ancient without a "before it was so." This is the Spirit that hovered over the waters on the day when God created all this (Gn 1:2). And because the Holy Spirit hovered over the waters, the waters were blessed and sanctified, and in them were born all those who have a living soul. As for us, we say therefore that those who are born with a second birth from water and the Holy Spirit (Jn 3:5), those who have received the earnest of the Holy Spirit (2Cor 1:22; 5:5), are alive in life (Ti 3:5). This is therefore the Spirit that spoke through the prophets and was proclaimed by the Apostles. Praise to the Trinity.

given the power of dominion over heaven and earth. Go therefore and teach all the Gentiles, and when you baptize them, say: in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit" (cf. Mt 28:18-19). 14 It is He who spoke to Moses and said to him: "I will come to you in a cloud of smoke, so that the people may hear while I speak with you and they may have faith in you, for ages of ages" (Ex 19:9). And again He said: "God descended upon Mount Sinai, upon the summit of the mountain" (Ex 19:20). And again He said to Moses: "Say to the house of Jacob and announce to the children of Israel that I have spoken to them from heaven so that they may not worship idols and nothing else besides me. For I am the Lord, I am your God" (Ex 20:22, 5). He said this to make us understand the faith in the one Trinity. He said in fact: "I am the Lord." Truly the Father is in the heavens above and on the earth below. In the sea and on the dry land, existing in everything everywhere, together with His Son and with His Holy Spirit. And it is He who said again: "your God," this regarding His Son, since He came into the world and became man, except only in sin (Heb 4:15). He has become ours and we (have become) His in holy baptism (Rm 6:5).

15 And as for what He said for the third time: "I am a jealous God" (Ex 20:5), (He said it) regarding His Holy Spirit because He is jealous and does not dwell where the spirit of the devil is (Wis 1:4). To say: "God," made us understand His Unity. And to say: "I am yours, I am jealous" (Ex 20:2, 5) indicates to us His Trinity. And as for what He said after He descended upon Mount Sinai: "I have spoken to him from heaven" (Ex 19:9) so that He might make us understand His omnipresence. Therefore, then, the devil and Pharaoh, idolatry and making bricks have been likened into one. And He who sent Moses and He who sent the Apostles is one God. The Father has therefore spoken through His Son and through His Holy Spirit has brought everything to completion. 16 And the exodus of Israel from making bricks and the exodus of the Gentiles from idolatry, this too is one. And for this reason He says: "The people will rejoice together with their peoples" (Ps 117:1). And when He wanted to redeem the world, the only Son came from the only Father, with the good pleasure of the only Holy Spirit. He bowed the heavens (Ps 17:10; 4Esd 1:18) and descended and came into the world, while being (already) in the world. And for this reason the evangelist John said: "And the light

of justice that illuminates all men, that comes into the world, is in the world, and even the world was made through Him" (Jn 1:9-10). 17 Yes, truly Jesus Christ is born of Mary, of the Holy Virgin, He who exists before the world was created (Jn 8:58), together with the Father in divinity. Equal in person and equal in form with Him who begat Him. The Father is not greater than His Son. And the Son is not smaller than His Father, and the Holy Spirit is equal with the Father and the Son. Trine, Holy, One, who is in three Persons. And He is Trine in one kingdom and one in one essence. From before, therefore, He is God, even now He is God, and He will be God until eternity. Blessed be His name and may His mercy be upon us, amen! The Gàdl of Fiqator Proemium 18 Adorned with this Trinity, satisfied with the honeycomb of the Mosaic law, suckled with the ten words, girded with the light of the flame, shod with the power of the Gospel, taking the armor of the faith, girding myself with the helmet of salvation (Eph 6:13, 15, 17), clothed with the magnificence of the divinity, loading myself with the cross of the Beloved with which I sign myself and defeat my enemy, drinking the medicine of the Prophets, crowning myself with the grace of the Apostles, praising the virginity of my Lady, one in body and another in thought, who know no error. Entrusting myself to these two of her virginities which were chosen, clothed with this power, I open my mouth. So that I may reveal and narrate the goodness of the struggle of Fiqator, my father.

I have searched for him here and there but I have not found him who is the support of my wavering. What do I have to do with you, O Thursday evening? For in you the Jews hastened to arrest my Lord and my Savior. And now in you has occurred the rest of the father of my spirit, and if I had not found in you the new mystery for my life, against you I would have multiplied my reproaches. How is it that the honeyed mouth is silent? How is it that the mouth of honey has been sealed?

20 His word and his message are preserved with me. And before I returned, they announced to me that he has emigrated from the country for a bitter death. To whom shall I expose the delicious words that I heard from Constantine, glorious pillar of the faith? I have lost, therefore, my father who raised me in love, meek and good, wise and learned, pure as gold and tested as silver, bound with virginity and tested with labor, pure and purified on earth. Defender of the faith, founded on the rock of the Gospel, may his blessing dwell in us, and may the word of his teaching remain with us, nourishing us with peace, amen.

It is You who bring out what is hidden and bring in what is outside; may the power of Your divinity speak through me. On how he was born and on how he grew and on how he had life through admonition and wisdom. And on how he was expert in listening to the word of the scriptures and was even more jealous in reading them, in learning them, and in keeping them. And taking from Him the good instruction, he loved what is good and repudiated what is bad.

look to pride and boasting. He joined himself to righteousness and did not turn toward error. He abhorred the mendacious word, and did not listen to the word of slander against an elder (1Tm 5:19). He was constant in remaining firm in the faith and always translated the Old and the New word of the divinity. In fact, the opening of his mouth was pleasant, and the sweetness of his word, and the grace of his voice, and the eloquence of his lips, according to what is written in the Gospel: "no man can take anything, absolutely nothing, unless it is given to him from heaven" (Jn 3:27).

23 And thus our father Fiqator welcomed the word like good soil that receives the seed, he bore fruit, and doubled and gave his fruit, instead of one, thirty, sixty, and a hundred (Mt 13:23). He resembled therefore the kingdom of heaven (represented by that) man who took a grain of mustard and sowed it in his field, (it) is smaller than all seeds and became a tree so large that the birds of the sky took shelter under it (Mt 13:31-32). And this our father Fiqator sprouted in humility and was raised in obedience and grew in the love of God and in the love of neighbor, so much so that his shadow covered the mountains and his branches extended over the sea and his sprout filled up to the rivers (Ps 79:11-12).

24 And his sprouts are like the cedar of God, and he has risen like the cedar of Lebanon (Ps 91:13), and he gave his fruit in his time (Ps 1:3) and the enemy did not plunder him and the birds of the sky did not devour him (Mt 13:3ff) and he became like an irrigated garden and like a well whose water does not extinguish (Is 58:11). He was planted in a moist land and he bore clusters of faith (Is 5:1ff). He blossomed (and gave) flowers of blessing each with its own odor, each with its own fragrance. A stream of water is the place in which his cluster sprouts; it is a cluster of mercy. And for this reason his leaves do not fall (Ps 1:3).

### Panegyric of Dàbrà Maryam
በአማን ፣ ሙሐዘ ፡ ማይ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ማርያም ። ፈልፈለ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፣ ምቅዳሐ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወብሉይ ፡ ገነት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ነቅዐ ፡ ገነት ፣ ዐዘቅተ ፡ ማየ ፣ ሕይወት ፡ ማኅደሮሙ ፡ ለንጹሐን ፣ ፈለገ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ የዐ ውዳ= ፣ ሞገደ ፣ ጸሎት ፣ ያመዓብላ ። ሰረገላ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ማእከላ ፣ ሐብለ ፣ ተአዝዞ ፣ ኢይሴስላ ፣ ተምኔተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ መሀይ
25 Dabrà Maryam is truly a stream of water. A source of books, (a place) in which the New and the Old (Testament) are copied, "it is a garden, source of the garden, well of the water of life, dwelling of the pure." The river of praise surrounds it, the wave of prayer moves it. The chariot of the angels moves in its midst. The chain of obedience does not depart from it. The aspiration of all the faithful is found in it. It renews the youth of the elders

ምናን ፡ ይትረከብ ፡ በላዕሌሃ ። ትሔድስ ፣ ውርዙቶሙ ፡ ለአእሩግ ፡ ለእለ ፣ ጸልኡ ፣ ዓለመ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፣ ወይከውኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ እለ ፡፣ አልቦሙ ፣ እከየ ፣ ወከመ ፣ ሐሊብ ፡ ዘአልቦ” ፣ ቱስሕተ ።
(Ps 102:5) who have hated the world (1Jn 2:15) and have come to it and will become like children (Mt 18:3) who have no malice and like milk that has no mixture (1Pt 2:1-2).

### Virtue of the monks of Dabrà Maryam

26 And when they flee from this passing world (1Cor 7:31; 1Jn 2:17) their life will be renewed. They will loosen their tongue for instruction and will examine the word of the Scriptures and of the Psalms of David with attention and will wash their sins with the source of hot tears, saying: "Woe to us then! Woe then to our souls! Where were we and toward where is our goal?" And they remembered their error of the past that they had committed in their youth (Jb 13:26). They insulted their flesh and humbled themselves and chained their limbs with (the works of) penance and with the bond of the Gospel, they dried up their belly with hunger and extinguished greed, they watched day and night and did not grant sleep to their eyes, nor rest to their eyelids, nor respite to their cheeks (Ps 131:4-5). As the Fathers say: "The vigil together with the hunger for bread dissolves the flesh." And here orphans are raised, those to whom their fathers and mothers have been lacking. And they become like the angels of God (Mt 22:30) and live by the power of the Holy Spirit. And they are watered with the eighty-one rivers, which are the New and Old Law. 27 And they delighted in the commandment (Ps 118:15) of life. But above all they distanced themselves from useless words (Mt 12:36) and there was no one who hated his brother and who cursed his neighbor. And there was no one who annoyed those who were greater than him. And when someone called another, the latter answered him with fear, saying: "Here I am, my lord!" In fact, they respected the elders greatly and had fear of gray hair. As for their teachers, (they considered them) as God, as the Apostles say in their Synod: "He who tells you the Word of God, he who for you has become a motive of life and has given you the ring of glory, love him as the pupil of your eye and remember him day and night and respect him as God. For the place in which they remember the divinity, God is there (Ex 3:5). Visit him every day, so that you may rest with the word of the remaining saints. And he who approaches the saints will become holy. And respect him in the measure of your strength, and with your labor, and with the work of your hands. For God has made you worthy to find with

28 Such is the disposition of our father Ewostatewos and of our father Absadi. They have planted their vineyard and have harvested their fruits, they have brought their sheaves and have returned to the heavenly Jerusalem, making joy (Ps 125:6). May the prayer and the blessing and the intercession of their children, great and small, become a shield of victory and a sword of help to our king Zàr'a Ya'eqob, for ages of ages, amen. Nativity and monastic profession of Fiqator 29 Let us begin therefore to proclaim the praise of the struggle of our father Fiqator, remnant of the Prophets and successor of the Apostles, may God give us his blessing, amen. And there was a man in the province of the east whose name was Betà Sàma'at, righteous and good and fearing God, whose name was Tàtàmqà Màdahn and the name of his wife was Amàtà Maryam. And the two were righteous before God. And this Tàtàmqà Madhan was zealous for justice like Elijah (1Kgs 21). And he begat two sons: the name of one was Fiqator and of the other Yonas. And before this our father Fiqetor knew good and evil (Rm 9:11), his father took him and led him to the country of the monks which is called Betà Gàbàz to Abba BàSàlotà Mikael, son of our father Ewostatewos, great priest, star of the church, who has not defiled his soul, parent of lions, fearsome lion, he has renewed Ethiopia with the beauty of his doctrine, he has clothed himself with humility. Who can complete his praise? Long live (be) his pilgrimage, (to him who) without fear spread his tunic, who of Lydda trampled the very middle of the belly. Reposed in

ውስተ ፣ አርማንያ ፣ ተዘግበ ፣ ለመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡፣ እንዘ ፣ የኅኀሥሣፆ ። ይዕቀበነ ፡ ዘዐቀቦ ፣ እንበለ ፡ አበሳ ፣ አሜን ። ንግባእ ፣ ኀበ ፣ ውዳሴሁ ፣ ለአፈ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ለምንኀረ” ፡ ጽድቅ ፣ እስመ ፣ ተሐፅነ ፣ ውስተ ፡፣ ቤተ ፣ መነኮሳት ፡፣ እምንእሱ ፡ ወነሥአ ፣ ግዝ ረተ ፡ በከመ ፣ ሕግ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ደብሩ ፣ ለበጸሎተ ፣ ሚካኤል ። ወተጠምቀ ፡ በስመ” ፣ ሥላሴ ፣ በእደዊሆሙ ፡ ለአርጋበ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ልህቀ ፡፣ በጥበብ ፡ ወጸንዐ ፣ በመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይረድኦ ፣ ኀይል ።
Armenia while he was seeking the kingdom of heaven (Mt 6:33). May He (also) guard us, He who guarded him without fault, amen. 30 We enter into the praise of him who has the golden mouth, channel of justice, since he was raised in the house of the monks (from) his youth and received circumcision according to the law, in the monastery of BaSalotà Mikael. He was baptized in the name of the Trinity by the hand of the doves of the Gospel (Mt 10:16), he grew in wisdom (Lk 2:52) and was firm in the Holy Spirit and for this reason the power helped him.

### Virtue and asceticism of Fiqator
ሐይወ ፣ በንጽሕ ፣ ወአፍቀራ ፡፣ ለትጋህ ፡፣ ወኮነ ፣ ንቁሀ ፣ ለሰሚዐ ፣ ቃለ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፣ ዘያበርህ ፡ ልበ ፣ ወያሥተፌስሕ ። ወኢተሐፅነኬ ፣ በተወል ውሎ ። የማነ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ሐፀነቶ ፣ ጥቅመ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፡ ዖደቶ" ። ሕገ ፣ ተላህዮ” ፤ ኢተምህረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ ውስተ ፣ መካነ ፣ መኳንንት ፣ ኢቆመ ፡ ከመ ፡ ልሂቅ ። ለዐቂበ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፡ ጽሁቅ ፣ ወለገቢሮቱ ፡፣ ጥንቁቅ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ለቢዎ ፡ ኩሎ ፡፣ ሕገ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈተወ ፡፣ ሥርዓተ ፣ ቀኖና ፡ ወለብሰ ፣ ልብሰ ፡፣ ምንኩስና ፡ ወሐይወ ፡ በድንግልና ። ተዘኪሮ ፣ ዘይቤ ፣ ጳውሎስ ፣ ዘአውሰበ ፣ ይሔሊ ፡ ንብረተ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ በዘ ፡ ያሠምራ”፤ ፣ ለብእሲቱ ። ወዘሰ ፡ ኢያውሰበ ፣ ይሔልዮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፣ በዘ ፣ ያሠምሮ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወዝንቱሰ ፣ አቡነ ፣ ኀጸወ” ፣ ርእሶ ፣
31 He lived in purity and loved vigilance and was solicitous in listening to the word of the Books that illuminate the heart and gladden. In fact, he was not raised in doubt. The right hand of God raised him, the bastion of the faith surrounded him. He did not learn the law of amusement like the boys. As an adult he did not stop in the houses of princes. He was diligent in keeping the faith and attentive in implementing it. And therefore, having understood all the law of God, he loved the canonical order, and wore the monastic habit and lived in virginity, mindful of what Paul said: "He who marries thinks of the way of living of this world, on how to please his wife. But he who does not marry will think of God, on how he will please God" (1Cor 7:33, 32). And this our father castrated himself for (the love of) God, as it was said in the Gospel: "And there are those who have castrated themselves for the kingdom of heaven" (Mt 19:12).
በእንተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ለሊ ሆሙ” ፣ ኀጸዉ” ፣ ርእሶሙ ፣ በእንተ ፣ መንግሥተ ፣ ሰማያት ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ አንጽሐ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፣ እምዝሙት ፡፣ ወርኩስ ፡ ወአጥፍአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡፣ አሕፃሁ ፣ ለእኩይ ፡ ርሱን ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ታቦቶ ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ቅንኡ ፡ ለእንተ ፡ ተዐቢ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትቴይስ ፣ ፍኖት ፡ እሜህረክሙ ። ወከማሁ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፣ እንተ ፣ ትቴይስ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ተምህረ ፣ ዘውስተ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡፣ ፍሕመ ፡ አቶ'ረረረ ፣ አርዑተ ፣ ድንግልና ፡ ጾረ ፣ ዘበምድር ፣ አስተሐቀረ ፣ ዘበሰማያት ፣ አብደረ ። ወእምዝ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ሶበ ፡ መጽኦ ፣ ሕሊና ፣ ፍትወተ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ አስ
32 For this, he purified his soul from lewdness and impurity and extinguished all the fiery darts of the evil one (Eph 6:16) and became the temple of the Holy Spirit (1Cor 6:19). As Paul says: "Be zealous for that greater grace and the better way I will teach you" (1Cor 12:31). And thus our father Fiqator learned the better way, he cooled the embers that were in his flesh, he endured the yoke of virginity. He despised earthly things and preferred heavenly ones (Col 3:2). After that, one day, when a thought of deceptive carnal passion came upon him, he took with his hand a coal of fire and placed it on his foreskin.
ፋጢ ፣ ነሥአ ፣ ትንታገ ፡፣ እሳት ፣ በእዴሁ ፡ ወአንበረ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ከተማ ፣ ነፍስቱ ። ርእዩ ፣ አኃውየ ፣ ጥብዐቶ ፡ ዘኢያፍርሆ ፡ ፍሕመ ፡፣ እሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውዒ ፣ ሥጋሁ ። እስመ ፣ መዓዛ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ኀደረ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወቦ ፡ እለሂ ፣ የሐይዉ ፡ ወቦ ፡፣ እለሂ ፡ ይትሐጐሉ ። እስመ ፣ መፀዛ ፣ ለእለ ፡ ሞት ፡ ውስተ ፣ ሞት ። ወቦ ፣ መፀዛ ፡ ለእለ ፡ እምሕይወት ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሕይወት ። ወለመኑ ፣ ይደልዎ ፡ ዝንቱ"?' ፣ እስመ ፣ ኢኮነ ፡ ከመ ፣ ብዙኃን ፣ እለ ፣ ይትሜየንዎ ፡ ለቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዳእሙ ፣ ከዊነነ” ፣ እምንጽሕ ፡ በዘ ፡ መሐረነ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ።
33 Observe, my brothers, his courage that was not intimidated by the burning ember, so much so as to burn his flesh. For the fragrance of Christ dwelt in him (2Cor 2:15). As Paul said: "And there are those who are saved and there are those who are lost. For it is an odor of death for those who are for death. And there is an odor for those who are from life for life. And to whom


[p. 184] {#po.51.p184}
በዝንቱ ፡ መዐዛ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ተምዒዓዞ ፣ መነነ” ፣ ለዓለም ፣ ሐውዞ ። ወአጥፍአ ፡ ለጸላኢ ፡ ሕምዞ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አሠነየ ፣ ግዕዞ ፣ እስመ ፣ ፍቅረ ፣ አምላክ ፣ አኀዞ ። ወኮነ ፣ ጸሀቄ” ፣ ለአንብቦ ፡ መጸሕፍት ፣ ወለፈክሮቶሙ ።
does this (fragrance) belong? For we are not like many of those who falsify the word of God, but we are pure according to what God taught us about Christ" (2Cor 2:15-17). Perfumed with this fragrance of life, he repudiated the pleasure of the world. And he destroyed the poison of the enemy. Therefore he made his conduct beautiful, for the love of God had in fact grasped him. And he was diligent in the reading of the Books and in their interpretation.
ወሶበሂ ፣ ያነብብ ፣ በውስተ ፣ ጉባኤ ፣ በኀበ ፣ መካነ ፣ ትምህርት ፡ ይሰ ማዕ ፡ እምርጐቅ ፡ እስመ ፡ ልዑለ ፡ ቃል ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ። ወያነክርዎ ፡ እለ ፡ ያጸምኡ ። እስመ ፣ ይበውእ ፣ ጉህና'፤ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ውስተ ፣ እዘኒሆሙ ። በከመ ፡ ተብህለ ፣ በሐዋርያት ፡፣ ወይኩን ፡ ዘያነብብ ፣ ሠናየ ፣ ዘይመልእ ፣ እዝነ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወዘያአምር ፡ መካነ ፡ ዘያነብብ ። ወይገብር ፣ በዘ ፡ አንበበ ። በዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፣ ኮነ ፣ ፍኖቱ ፣ ወሑረቱ ። ወኢያጸርዕ ፣ ገዬሰ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወኢያበጥል ፡ ሰዓታተ ፣ ሌሊት ፣ ወመዓ ልት ። ወሶበ ፡፣ ይቀውም ፡ ለጸሎት ፡ ስፍሕት"= ፡ እዴሁ ፣ እስመ ፡ ያወ ትር ፡ በስኢል ፣ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በውዑይ ፡ ልብ ፡ ወአልቦቱ ፡ ትካዘ ፣ ዝዓለም ፣ አላ ፣ ትካዙ ፣ ወሕሊናሁ ፡ ለአሠንዮ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወለተ አዝዞ ፣ ቃለ ፡ መምህሩ ። ወእንዘ ፣ ሀለወ ፡ በዘ ፣ ከመዝ ፣ ግብር ፡ ርእየ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ እንዘ ፡፣ ይጸውሩ ፡ ዐምደ ፡፣ ለሕንጸተ ፡፣ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ርእያ ፡ ለእግ ዝእትነ ፣ ማርያም ፡፣ እንዘ ፡ ትእኅዞ ፣ ለዐምድ ፡ በጻዕዳ ፡ ፈትል ፡ ወያን ሶሱ ፣ በማእከል ። ወእለ ፡ ይጸውርዎ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኢይበጽሑ ፡፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ ለዐ ምድ ፣ ወዐምድኒ ፣ ኢይበጽሕ ፣ ኀበ ፣ አርእስቲሆሙ ፣ ወየሐውሩ ፡ ርቱዐ ፣ ትሬእዩኑ ፣ ኦአኃዊየ ፡ ዘከመዝ ፣ ጸጋ ፡ ዘተውህበ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ድርዐ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ዑጽፍ ፣ ዘየሐውር ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፣ በግብረ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሱቱፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወርቅ ፣ ንጡፍ ፣ ነጊረ ፣ ግብሩ ፣ ትሩፍ ፣ ወስእለቱኒ ፡ ውኩፍ ፣ ኀይለ ፣ ረድኤቱ ፡ ዲቤነ ፡ ያዕርፍ ። ወካዕበ ፡፣ እንዘ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ በቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፣ በጊዜ ፡ ሞርርባን ፡ ይቤ ፣ ርኢኩ ፣ መጽአ ፣ ኅቤየ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ፍሥሕ ፣ አኀዘ ፡ ልብስየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይት ዋነይ ፣ ምስሌየ ፣ ወአንቃዕደወ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወጐንደየ ፣
34 And when he read in the assembly, in the place of instruction, one heard him from afar because he had a high voice like Isaiah. And those who listened (to him) admired him. In fact, his arcane voice penetrated their ears, according to what had been said by the Apostles: "And let him who reads be good, who fills the ears of the people and who knows the passage he is reading and who practices what he has read." His path and his way was in all this. He did not fail (ever) to go to church and did not interrupt the hours of the night and of the day. And when he stood for prayer, his hand was extended because he was assiduous in the supplication before God with a warm heart and he had no worries of this world, but his worry and his thought was turned to the benefit of the church and to obedience to the word of his master. Visions of Fiqator 35 And while he was in such a situation, one day he saw that they were transporting a column to build a church, (and) he saw our Lady Mary, while she was holding the column with a white thread and was moving in the midst. And those saints who were carrying (it) did not reach the column and the column did not reach their heads and they walked straight. See, my brothers, how such a grace was granted to our father Fiqator? Clothed with the breastplate of the faith, who walks according to what is written, participant in the work of the saints, tested like gold, to tell of his work is superabundant, his prayer is welcomed, may the power of his help rest upon us. Again

ነዋኀ ፣ ሰዓተ ፣ ወእምዝ ፣፡ አምኀኒ ፡ በአፉሁ ፡ ክብርት ። ነጽሩ ፡ እስኩ ፣ ዘከመዝ ፡ ዕበየ ፣ ለስዒመ ፣ መድኅን ፣ ዘተኅርየ ፣ በሐሊበ ፣ ወንጌል ፡ ተሰቅየ ፣ በሥርዓተ ፣ አብጥሊስ ፡ ተቀንየ ፣ እምሐውዘ ፣ ዓለም ፡ ዘጐየ ፣ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ አጥረየ ፣ ካዕበተ ፡ መንፈሱ ፣ ይትመከዐብ ፣ ዲቤነ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፣ ሰማየ ።
while he was standing in church during the Eucharist he said: "I saw that a joyful child came to me, he took my garment as if he were playing with me, and he looked toward me and lingered for a long hour, after which he greeted me with his glorious mouth." Look therefore at such greatness! He was chosen to kiss the Savior, he was watered with the milk of the Gospel, he submitted himself to the disposition of the Apostolic Canons, he fled from worldly pleasure, he acquired the kingdom of heaven, may the double of his spirit that is in heaven be doubled upon us (2Kgs 2:9)!
ወካዕበ ፡፣ ንዜንወክሙ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ ንነግር ፡ ዘሰማዕነ ፡ እስመ ፣ ጸሎቱ ፣ ይረድአነ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ከመ ፣ ይሑር ፣ ፍኖተ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ርእየ ፡ ራእየ ፡ ዘያበርህ ፡ ልበ ፡ ወያስተፌሥሕ ፣ ነፍሰ ፡ አይድዓኒ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፣ መሠጡኒ ፣ ላዕለ ፡ ወአ ብኡኒ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፣ ሰማያዊት ፡ ወአብጽሑኒ ፣ ኅበ ፣ ሀለወት ፣ ታቦት ፡፣ ስርጉት ፣ ወመልዕልቴሃ ፡ አጎበር ፡፣ በአምሳለ ፡ ቀስተ ፡ ደመና ። ወጽሑፍ ፡ ህየ ፡፣ አስማተ ፣ ፲ ፣ ወ፪ ፣ ሐዋርያተ ፡ በግዑ ። ወሰማዕኩ ፡ ቃለ ፣ ዘይብል ፡ ጸሐፎ ፡ ለፊቅጦር ፣ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ በቀለመ ፡ ብርሃነን ፡ እንዘ ፣ እሬኢ ፡ በአዕይንትየ ። ናሁ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ መንክራቲከ ፣ እግዚኦ ፡ በከመ ፣ አይዳዕኮ ፣ ለወልደ ፣ ነጐድጓድ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ለዘ ፡ ሞአ ፡ እሁቦ ፡፣ መጽሐፈ ፡፣ ብርሃን ፣ ወውስቴቱ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ስም ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ዘአ ልቦ ፣ ዘየአምሮ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘነሥኦ ። ብፁዕ ፡ ዘኀረይኮ ፣ ወዘተወከፍኮ ። ወዘአኅደርኮ ፣ ውስተ ፣ አዕፃዲከ ። አማን ፣ ብፁዕ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅ ጦር ፣ እንዘ ፣ ሀሎ ፣ በሕይወቱ ፣ ዘርእየ ፡ ዕሜቶ ፣፡ ጸሎቱ ፣ ወስእለቱ ፡ ያብአነ ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ በአቱ ፡ አሜን ።
36 Again we tell you what happened, according to what was said: "We tell what we have heard" (1Jn 1:1), because his prayer protects us. After that, when our father Fiqator thought of setting out on the journey toward Jerusalem, he saw a vision that illuminates the heart and gladdens the soul, he told me saying: "They snatched me on high and introduced me into the heavenly Jerusalem and made me arrive (at the place) where there was the adorned ark and above it a lid in the shape of a rainbow. And there was written the name of the twelve Apostles of the Lamb (Rev 21:14). And I heard a voice that said: 'Write Fiqator together with them, with the ink of light while I look with my eyes'." Behold, they have been fulfilled, O Lord, your portents according to what you said to the son of thunder, when you said: "To him who overcomes I will give the book of light and inside it is written a new name that no one knows except him who has received it" (Rev 2:17). Blessed is he whom you have chosen and accepted, and whom you have made to dwell in your courts (Ps 64:5). Our father Fiqator is truly blessed who saw his reward while he was alive. May his prayer and his intercession introduce us where he has entered, amen. Testimony on the piety of Fiqator
ወካዕበ" ፣ አይድዐ ፣ ፳ ፡ ብአሲ ፡ ዲያቆን ፣ ዘስሙ ፡፣ ይስሐቅ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ አንሰ ፣ ርኢክዎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይቄድስ ፡ ቀርባነ ፡ ለቢሶ ፣ ልብሰ ፡ ክህነት ፡ ወያቄርብ ፡ ቀኑርባነ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፣፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ ጸዊሮ ፡ ማዕተበ ፡ መስቀል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይቀውም ፣ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ወይትናገር ፡ በኀይል ። ሥልሰመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሊተሰ ፡ ወሰዱኒ ፣፡ መንገለ ፣ የማን ፡ ወናሁ ፣ እመውት ፣ ሀለውኩ ፣ ወለአቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦርሰ ፡ ማእከለ ፣ ብዙኀን ፣ አንበርዎ ። አማነ ፣ ርቱዐ” ፣ ይደልዎ ፣ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ጳውሎስ ፡ ኅሬት ፣፡ አድምዐቶ ፣ ለተልእኮ ፡ ምሥጢር ፡፣ አስተዳለወቶ ። እንዘ ፣ ሀሎ ፡ ሕያወ ፣ ቅድመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣ ቀዊመ ፡ ጸገወቶ ።
37 And again a man, a deacon, by the name of Yashaq proclaimed saying: "I have seen our father Fiqator while he was sanctifying the Eucharist, clothed with the priestly vestments and offering the Eucharist." And again he said: "I saw him carrying the sign of the cross while he stood before God and spoke with strength." For the third time he said: "As for me, they carried me toward the right and behold I was about to die but they placed our father Fiqator in the midst of a multitude." Truly and justly he is worthy, as Paul said (Rm 11:5, 7, 27), the election has munificated him for the service of the mystery. While still being alive,


[p. 188] {#po.51.p188}
አበ ፣ ለብዙኃን ፣ ሜጫመቶ ፣ እምጥበበ ፣ ዓለም ፡ ሰወረቶ ፣ ኀበ ፣ ጥበብ ፣ መንፈሳዊ ፣ ሜጠቶ ። ይኅርየነ ፣ ዘኀረዮ ፣ እንበለ ፡ አስትቶ ፡ አሜን ።
He granted him to stand before God. He constituted him father of a multitude (Gn 17:5). He safeguarded him from the wisdom of this world (1Cor 1:20), he led him to spiritual wisdom. May He choose (also) us, without contempt, He who chose him, amen.

### To Hayq to seek the book of Baruch
ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ኢያጽህቆ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፣ ዘእንበለ ፣ ኀሚሠ ፣ ቃለ ፣ መጻሕፍት ። ወሶበ ፡፣ ዜነውዎ ፣ ከመ ፡ሀሎ ፣ መጽሐፈ ፡፣ ባሮክ ፣ ወፅአ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይኅሥሥ ፣ ወያደ፤ ፣ ኩሎ ፣ መካናተ ፣ እስከ ፣ ሐይቅ ፡፣ ወሴዋ ፣ ርእዩ ፣ መጠነ ፣ ያፈቅሮ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፣፡ ኢያ ፍርሆ ፣ ጻማ ፣ ፍኖት ፡ ወኢዋዕየ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ እንዘዎ ፡ የኀሥሥ ፡ ፡ መጽ ሐፈ ፣ ዘኅጥአ ። ወዓቃቤ ፡ ሰዓትሰ ፣ ብንያም ፡ ሶበ ፡ ነጸረ ፣ ሥነ ፣ ግብሩ ፡ ወጣዕመ ፣ ነገሩ ፡ አፍቀሮ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ እስመ ፣ መፍቀሬ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ወኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ሰምዕዎ ፡ ያነክርዎ ፡ ወያፀምእዎ” ፣ በአርምሞ ፣ እስመ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ቃለ ፡ መለኮት ፣ ወኢይደግሙ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ነገሩ ። በከመ ፡፣ ይቤ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ኪያየ ፡ ያፀምኡ” ፡ ወይሰምዑ ፡ ወያረምሙ ፡ በምክረ ፣ ዚአየ ፡ ወኢይደግሙ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ነገርየ ። ወይትሐሠዩ ፣ ሶበ ፡ እትናገሮሙ ፣ ከመ ፣ ምድር ፡ ጽምእት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትሴፎ ፣ ዝናመ ፡ ከማሁ ፣ እሙንቱሂ ፣ ለነገረ ፡፣ ዚአየ ። ። ወዝንቱሰ ፣ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ያስተፌሥሕ ፡ ከመ ፣ ወይን ፣ ነገሩ ፡ ያጠልል ፡ ከመ ፣ ቅብእ ፡፣ ወያጸግብ ፡ ከመ ፡ እክል ፣ ወይት ፈቶ ፣ እምወርቅ ፣ ወእምዕንቀ፦ ፣ ክቡር ። ወይጥዕም ፣ እመዓር ፡ ወሦከር ፡ እስመ ፣ ይሜርሖ” ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ በኩሉ ፡ ፍኖቱ ።
38 And this our father Fiqator did not crave the wealth of this world, if not the investigation of the word of the (sacred) Books. And when they announced to him that there was the book of Baruch, he went out to seek it and wandered through all the places up to Hayq and to Sewa. Look at the measure to which he loved God! The fatigue of the journey did not intimidate him, nor the scorching of the sun when he was seeking a single book that he was missing. And when the aqqabe sà'at Binyam verified his good conduct and the goodness of his eloquence, he loved him greatly because he was a lover of God and all those who had heard him admired him and listened to him in silence because he pronounced the word of the divinity and they had nothing to say against his word. As Job says: "They offered me the ear and listened to me and were silent for my counsel and had nothing to say against my word" (Jb 29:21-22). And when I spoke to them, they rejoiced like thirsty land that hoped for rain, so they (longed for) my word. And also the word of this our father Fiqator gladdened like wine, his word and fattened like oil and satisfied like food and was more lovable than gold and the precious pearl. And it was sweeter than the honeycomb and honey (Ps 18:11) because God accompanied him in every way of his.
ወእንዘ ፣ ሀሎ ፡ ምድረ ፣ ሴዋ ፡ ርእየ ፡፣ ፈረሰ ፣ ቀይሐ ፡ ወምዩጥ ፣፡ ገጹ ፣ መንገለ ፣ ምዕራብ ፡ ወሰምዐ ፣ ቃለ ፣ ዘይብል ፡ እቱ ፡ ብሔረከ ፡ ወአበየ ፣ አቲወ ፣ እስመ ፣ ተሰናአልዎ ፡ ሰብአ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ብሔር ፣ ወይ ፈቅዱ ፡ ይጽግቡ ፣ ሥነ ፡ ትምህርቱ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኢክህለ ፣ ሐዊረ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፣ ካዕበ ፣ ብእሲ ፣ ግሩም ፣ ወቀዬሕ ፣ ራእዩ ፡ ወዕውድ” ፡ በጽ ሕም ፣ ወልሑይ” ፣ ገጹ ፣ ወጽዱላት ፣ አዕይንቲሁ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡ እቱ ፡ ሀገረከ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፣ አውሥአ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፣ አን ተኬ ፡ ተአቱኑ ፣ ምስሌየ ፣ ወይቤሎ ፣ እወ ፡ አአቱ ፣ ምስሌከ ። ወሶ ቤሃ ፡ ነገሮ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ለርድኡ ። ተንሥኡ ፡ በጊዜሃ ፣፡ ወኢኀደሩ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ እስመ ፡ አእመረ ፣ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘአስተርአዮ ፣ ረኣዬ” ፣ ኅቡአት ፣ አቡነ ፣ አብሳዲ ፡፣ በሰረገለ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ዓዋዲ"" ፣ ለሐውጸ ፡ አባግዒሁ ፣ ነጋዲ፤ ፣ አንገፎሙ ፣ እምሀያዲ ፣ ወቤዘዎሙ ፡፣ እምረዋዲ ፣ ዘአጽርዐ ፣ ትምህርተ ፣ ንስጥሮስ ፣ ከሐዲ ፡፣ ኢኀደጎሙ ፣ ውስተ ፣ ባሕረ ፡ ዓለም ፣ አባዲ ፣
In a vision, Absadi orders Fiqator to return to his original monastery. 39 And while he was in the region of Sewa, he saw a red horse with its face turned toward the west, and he heard a word saying: "Return to your country!" But he refused, because the people of that country were in agreement with him and desired to be satisfied with the goodness of his teaching. And for this reason, he could not go. And again, a fearsome man appeared to him, of clear appearance, surrounded by a beard, with a graceful face (Ct 2:14) and luminous eyes, and said to him: "Return to your country!" But this father of ours, Fiqator, answered saying: "Will you also return with me?" And he said to him: "Certainly, I will return with you." And immediately (Fiqator) told this to his disciple. They rose up at once and did not remain there, because he had realized that the man who had appeared to him was the seer of hidden things, our father Absadi, he who circulated with the chariot of the spirit, the wanderer who visits his sheep, who freed them from the bird of prey and redeemed them from the aggressor, who destroyed the doctrine of Nestorius the traitor, who did not abandon them in the foolish sea of the world; may his prayer and his intercession visit us and may he again compensate for our errors with the purity of his work. Fiqator is arrested by "remnants of the Jews."

40 We proceed then to proclaim the praise of our father Fiqator. While he was on the way to return to his country as he had been commanded, when he reached the region of Simen near the snowy heights, evil peoples, remnants of the Jews, arrested them, made them stay in a house, set guards over them, barred the whole house, and desired to kill them. Indeed, our father Fiqator had already contradicted the people of that country, saying: "Everything that the Prophets had prophesied has been fulfilled. Isaiah had indeed said: 'Behold, the virgin shall conceive and bear a son and shall call his name Emmanuel' (Is 7:14). And again he said: 'A child is born to us, a son is given to us, [the sovereignty is upon his shoulders, his name shall be called: Wonderful Counselor, Mighty God, because

I will bring peace to the leaders. And his life is great before him, and there will be no end to his peace. And upon the throne of David his kingdom will remain firm. And he will receive (it) in justice and righteousness, from now and until the end' (Is 9:5-6). Jeremiah says: 'Glorify God. And all of you, glorify the Messiah, Son of God, who awakens you and judges you, Jesus, Son of God. Light of all the world, lamp that does not go out, life of faith' (Ethiopic ParJer 11:45-47)". He cited this and other things similar to it from the books of the Prophets and recited it all according to its order. And they also answered him according to the measure of their capacity. But he hindered them with the power of the books and defeated them with justice, and for this reason they wanted to kill him, but the power of God saved them. Fiqator escapes miraculously from the prison of the "Jews." 41 And he led them out by night while (the guards) were watching them, surrounding them (Acts 12:7-10). And they passed through the snow, and on that road there was a hard battle. And while they were walking, two men appeared to them under the guise of monks and said to them: "Peace be with you!". And the saint answered and said to them: "The peace of God be with you!". And those men said to him: "Where have you come from, brothers, and where are you going?". The saint said to them: "We have come from over there and are going over there, because our country is the country of Sarawe. And we are children of Ma'ageba Egzi'e, children of Abba Tawalda-Madahn of Dabra Maryam.".
ለአባ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ መድኅን ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ ማርያም ። ወአውሥኡ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ ፅደው ፣ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፣ ኢያእመርክሙኑ ፡ ውስተዝ ፣ ፍኖት ፡ ከመ ፣ ሀሎ ፣ ቀትል ፡ ወእፎ ፡፣ ደፈርክሙ ። ወአው ሥአ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፣ እንዘ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፣ ምስሌነ ፣ ምንትኑ ፡ ያፈር ሀነ ፣ አኮኑ ፣ ጽሑፍ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ አይቴኑ ፡ አሐውር ፡ እመንፈስከ ። ወአይቴ ፡ እጐይይ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ገጽከ ። እመኒ ፡ ዐረጉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፣ ህየኒ ፡ አንተ ። ወእመኒ ፡ ወረድኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ ህየኒ ፡ ሀሎከ ። ወእ መኒ ፣ ነሣእኩ ፡ ክንፈ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንስር ። ወሰረርኩ ፡ እስከ ፡ ማሕለቅተ ፣ ባሕር ። ህየኒ ፣ እዴከ ፣ ትመርሃኒ ። ወታነብረኒ ፣ የማንከ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ምስሌሁ ፣ ምንት ፡ ያፈርሆ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ እል ክቱ ፡ ዕደው ፣ ተፈሥሑ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ነገር ፡፣ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ጽንዐ ፡ ሃይ ማኖት ።
42 And those men answered and said to the saint: "Did you not know that along this road there is a hard battle? How then did you dare?". And the saint answered and said to them: "What then can make us afraid while Christ is with us (Rom 8:35-39)? Is there not the writing that says: 'Where then can I go from your spirit? And where can I flee from your presence? If I ascend into heaven, you are there. And if I descend into the abyss, you are there. And even if I took the wings like an eagle and flew to the end of the sea, even there, your hand leads me, your right hand makes me stand (firm)' (Ps 138:8-10). And even now, what can frighten him who has God with him?". And having heard (this), those men rejoiced at this speech when they saw the firmness of (his) faith.
ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አንትሙሰ ፡ አርምሙ ፡ ቃለክሙ ፡ እስመ ፣ ኢተ አምሩ ፡፣ ልሳነ ፣ ብሔር ፣ ወናሁ ፣ ንሕነ ፡ ንመርሐክሙ ፡ በፈቃደ ፡፣ እግዚ
43 And they said to the saint: "You then, [do not speak a word because you do not know the language of the country; and behold! we will lead you by the will

አብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ አውፅእዎሙ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ወይቤልዎሙ ፡ ሑሩ" ፣ እንተ ፣ መንጸርክሙ ፣ ሀገር ፣ ነዋ ፣ ጥቃክሙ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይረ ክበክሙ፤፤ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሠናይ ። ወአዕረፉ ፡ ህየ ፡ እስመ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዕረፍት ፣ ዘሰኖብት ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አልቦ ፣ ንመጽእ ፡ ምስ ሌክሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገርክሙ ፣ ይቴይሰነ ፡ መጺእ” ። ወሰሐቅዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፣ ብሔርነሰ ፡ ርቅ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ወኢይትከሀለክሙ ፣ ትብጽሑ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ምስ ሌነ ፣ ወአንትሙሰ ፣ ሑሩ" ፣ ኀበ ፣ ዘአመርናክሙ ፡፣ ወሰላመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ የሀሉ ፣ ምስሌክሙ ።
of God!". And after that, they led them out of that road of brigandage and said to them: "Go to the country in front of you! Behold, it is near you! And nothing evil will meet you, only good!". And there they rested because the time of the rest of the Sabbaths had arrived. And the saint said to them: "Are we not coming with you? It is better for us to come to your country!". And they laughed at him and said to him: "Unfortunately, our country is far away and now you cannot reach it together with us; you, however, go where we have indicated to you, and the peace of God be with you".

### The heavenly guides depart from Fiqator and companions
ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎሙ ፡፣ ኅለፉ ፣ እልክቱ ፡ ዕደው ፡፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወቅዱ ስኒ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ አተዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ። ወአእመረ ፡ በመ ንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ ከመ ፣ ዝንቱ ፣ ግብር ፡ እምኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኮነ ። ወፈነዎሙ ፣ ለመላእክቲሁ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ዕደው ፡ ከመ ፣ ይርድእዎሙ ፡ ወያድኅንዎሙ ፣ እምቀትል ። ወናሁ ፣ ተፈጸመ ፣ ቃለ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘአምላከ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ረዳኢሁ ። ወትውክልቱኒ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ አምላኩ ። ወዓዲ ፣ ይቤ ፣ እስመ ፣ ለመላእክቲሁ ፡ ይኤዝ ዞሙ ፣ በእንቲአከ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይዕቀቡከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ፍናዊከ ።
44 And having said this, those men passed beyond them, while the saint together with his son returned to that country. And Fiqator knew in the Holy Spirit that this work had been brought about by God. And He sent His angels under the guise of men, so that they might help them and save them from the battle. And behold, the word of the Book has been fulfilled which says: "Blessed is the man who has the God of Jacob as his help, whose hope is in the Lord his God" (Ps 145:5). And it also says: "For He will command His angels for you, so that they may preserve you in all your ways" (Ps 90:11).

### Fiqator returns to Dabra Maryam

45 And after all this, they returned to the hermitage, their dwelling, Dabra Maryam. And the saint, according to his habit, submitted himself to the reading of the Books, and his reading was (done) with attention. As the prophet Daniel says: "(He) who stands in the holy place and who reads, let him be attentive" (Dn 9:23). And the apostle also said: "As for those who read and do not pay attention, they resemble the wind and like a country in which there is no one". Chronological review. 46 And after this, in those days reigned Zar'a Ya'eqob, lover of God, of the right faith. And the years of the world that have passed from Adam until the first year of the reign of Zar'a Ya'eqob, nicknamed Constantine, (are) nine hundred according to the cycle of weeks with four hundred years, so it seems. Three hundred and fifty-two together with seven years, according to the calculations of the cycle of the computation. So it seems. And furthermore, the Year of Mercy that approaches the Ga'az computation: seventy-seven with twelve years. It seems so, from so, until

so. I do not know about the coming years, as Ezra says: "I know about what has passed, but I do not know about what is to come" (4 Esd 4:46). But He who appeared to him said to him: "Announce this word and hide this word" (4 Esd 14:6).

### The reign of Zar'a Ya'aqob
ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ነግሠ ፣ ዘርአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ዘተሰምየ ፣ ቁስጣንጢኖስ ፡፣ በታስዕ ፣ ወርኅ ፡ እምአውራኀ ፣ ሮሜ ፡ ወዐሥር ፡ በግዕዝ ፡ ወሳድስ ፡ በጽ ርፅ ። ወግልስ ፣ በዕብራውያን ። ወአመ” ፣ ፳ ፣ ሁ ፡ ለሐዚራን ፣ ወዝው እቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወ፳ ፡ ለሰኔ” ፣ በግፅዝ ፣ ሐለየ ፡ በልቡ ፡፣ ዘይቤ ፡ ሰሎ ሞን ፣ ቀዳሚሃ ፣ ለጥበብ ፣ ፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወምክር ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘይገብራ ። ወበካልእኒ ፣ መጽሐፍ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሥርዋ ፡ ለጥበብ ፡ ፈሪሃ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ወአዕጹቂሃኒ ፣ ያነውኅ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወት ። ወሐ ዋርያ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ጥበቡ ፣ እንከ ፣ ወአጥብቡ ፡ ልበክሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወዘይመስሎ ፡፣ ለበወ ፣ ብዙኃ ። እስመ ፣ ለሊሁ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ምሁረ ፡፣ መጻ ሕፍት ፣ ወትርጓሜሆሙ ፣ ወፍካሬሆሙ ። ወሐለየ ፡ ዘይቤ ፣ ሰሎሞን ፣ ብዝኀ ፣ ጠቢባን” ፣ መድኃኒተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወንጉሥ ፣ ጠቢብ” ፡ መድኀኒተ ፡ ሕዝብ ። ወለበወ ፡፣ ዘይቤ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ጠቢብ ፣ ይጌሥጽ ፣ አሕዛቢሁ ፣ መኩንን ፡ ለባዊ ፣ ይገብር ፣ ሥርዓተ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ነገር ፡ ተመስጠ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ በፍቅረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወበ ጣዕመ ፡ ቃለ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፣ ኮነ ፡ ስቁየ” ። እስመ ፣ ሰምዐ ፣ ዜናሆሙ ፣ እምቅድመ ፣ ይንግሥ ፡ ለእሉ ፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአባ ፣ መዐቅባ ፡ እግ ዚእ ፣ ዘተሰምየ ፣ ኤዎስጣቴዎስ” ፣ ወዘከመ ፣ የሐውሩ ፡ በሥርዓተ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ዘሐዋርያት ፣ ወያቀውሙ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ በ ወ8 ፣ መጻሕፍት ። ወዘከመ ፡ ያከ ብሩ ፣ ሰንበታተ ፡ ወበዓላተ ፣ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ወበዝ ፣ ነገር ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ፈድፋደ ።
47 After this, when Zar'a Ya'eqob, nicknamed Constantine, reigned, in the ninth month of the Roman calendar, in the tenth in the Ga'az one, and in the sixth in the Greek one, and in the third in the Hebrew one, on the twentieth of Haziran, that is, on the twenty-sixth of Sane in the Ga'az (calendar), he thought in his heart about what Solomon said: "The beginning of wisdom is the fear of God" (Prv 1:7), and it is a good counsel to those who fulfill it (Ps 110:10). And in another book it says: "The root of wisdom is the fear of God, and its branches lengthen the days of life" (Sir 1:25). And the apostle Peter says: "Be therefore wise and sober your hearts" (1 Pt 5:8). He paid much attention to this and other things similar to it. Because the king himself was instructed in the Books and in their translation and in their explanation. 48 And he thought about what Solomon had said: "A multitude of wise men is medicine for the world, and a wise king is medicine for the people" (Wis 6:26). And he paid attention to what (Solomon) said: "A wise king admonishes his peoples, and the sensible governor will set order" (Sir 10:1). And because of this word, his thought was rapt by the love of God. And he was watered with the sweetness of the word of the Scriptures. Because he had heard the fame of those holy children of Abba Ma'aqaba Egzi'e, nicknamed Ewostatewos, before he became king. And about how they walked according to the canonical order of the apostles and how they brought forward testimonies from the eighty-one Books. And about how they celebrated the Sabbaths and the feasts according to what is written, and for this thing he rejoiced greatly.


[p. 198] {#po.51.p198}

### Zar'a Ya'aqob sends an invitation to the saints
ወእምድኅረ ፣ ነግሠ ፣ በሣልስ ፣ ወርኅ ፡፣ ለአከ ፣ ኀበ ፣ እሉ ፡ ቅዱ ሳን ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሰላምአ” ፣ ለክሙ ፣ በአምኃ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወናሁአ ፣ አነአ ፣ ሶበአ ፣ ሰማዕኩአ ፣ ዜናክሙአ ፡፣ አፍቀርኩአ ፣ እስመ ፡ ተሐውሩአ ፡ በሕገ ፣ መጻሕፍትአ ። ወይእዜኒአ ፡ ፈንዉ ፣ ኅቤየአ ፡ ሰብአአ ፡ ጠቢባነአ ፡ እለ ፣ ይተረጉጐሙአ ፡ መጻሕፍተአ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይርታዕ ፣ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቲያንአ ። ወጸልዩአ ፣ ላዕሌየአ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ መንግሥትየ ፡ አ ። ወበጺሖ ፡ ላእክ ፣ ነገረ ፡ ለጉባኤ ፣ ዘደብረ ፣ ማርያም ፡ ወለአባ ፣ ተወልደ ፣ መድኅን ። ወሶበ ፣ ሰምዐ ፣ አረጋዊ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘይቤ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ በጭወጵ፳ መጻሕፍተ ፣ ሕግ ፣ ንትዋሣእ ። ወደቂቁኒ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አኃው ፡ ንምከርኬ ፡ ዘናገብእ ፣ ቃለ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አረጋዊ ፣ ምንተኑ ፡ ዘንመክር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኦሆ ፣ ወሐዊር ፡ ፍጡነ ።
49 And three months after he reigned, he sent (a message) to those saints saying: "May peace be with you through the gift of the Holy Spirit! And behold, when I heard news about you, I loved (you) because you walk according to the law of the Books. And now, send therefore to me wise people who interpret the books so that the Christian faith may be rectified. And pray for me and for my kingdom!". And the messenger arrived (at his destination) and reported to the assembly of Dabra Maryam and to Abba Tawalda-Madahn. And when the old man heard what the king had said: "Let us discuss on the basis of the law of the eighty-one Books!", he rejoiced greatly. And his children also rejoiced with him, and the brothers said to him: "Let us consult then on the word with which we must answer the king". And the old man said to them: "What must we consult about the right faith, if not (to say) yes and set out promptly?". The assembly accepts the king's invitation.
ወሠምሩ ፡ ጉባኤ ፣ በዝ ፣ ነገር ፡ ወአግብኡ ፣ ቃለ ፣ ቡራኬ ፡ ለን ጉሥ ፣ ወተናገርዎ ፡፣ ለላእክ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይብሉ ፡ ኦሆ ፣ በቃለ ፣ ሰላም ። ወእ ምዝ ፣ ኅረዩ ፣ እምአኃው ፡ ጠቢባነ ፡ ወኀረዮ ፡ አባ ፣ ተወልደ ፣ መድኅን ፡ ለብፁዕ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይሑር” ፡ ኅበ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ እስመ ፣ ጠቢብ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወምሁር ፡፣ መጻሕፍተ ፣ ነቢያት ፣ ወሐዋርያት ፣ ወትርጓሜሆሙ ። ወፈነዎ ፣ ምስለ ፡፣ አኃዊነ ፣ ኀበ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ሰላም ።
50 And the assembly agreed on this word and answered the king with a word of blessing and spoke to the messenger saying: "It is well with the word of peace!". And after that, they chose wise men among the brothers, and Abba Tawalda-Madahn chose the blessed Fiqator so that he might go to the king, because he was very wise and instructed in the books of the Prophets and of the Apostles and in their interpretation. And he sent him to the king together with our brothers with a word of peace.

### Observance of the Sabbaths, even to giving one's own life
ወእሉሰ ፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ ደቂቁ ፡፣ ለአባ ፡ ማዕቃባ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ዘተሰምየ ፡ ኤዎስጣቴዎስ ፣ ወቦሙ ፣ ልማደ ፣ ሕግ ፡ ወቀኖና ፡ በእንተ ፣ ክብረ ፡ ሰንበ ታት ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ዘየሐውሩ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ እምከመ ፡ አልጸቀ ፡ ሳድስት ፡ ዕለት ፣ ወመስየ ፣ ኢይትገሐሱ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡፣ ኢለየማን ፡ ወኢለፀጋም ፣ ዳእሙ ፡፣፡ ህየ ፡ ያሰነብቱ ። እመሂ ፣ ብሔረ ፡ ቀትል ፣ ወፈያት ፣ ወእመሂ ፡ ምድረ ፡ አራዊት ፡፣ ወእመሂ ፣ ኮነ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሞት ፡ ወለእመሂ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ምድረ ፡ መቅሠፍት ፣ ወሕማም ፡ ወመዓት ። አው ፣ ምድረ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ወጽምእ ፡ ወእ መሂ ፡ በዘ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ከዊኖ ፣ አልቦ ፡ ኀበ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሀየ ፡ ያሰነ ብቱ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብሉ ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ይመውቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአ
51 As for those saints, children of Abba Ma'aqaba Egzi'e, called Ewostatewos, they had the custom of the law and the canon regarding the veneration of the Sabbaths, and regarding their traveling, that if the sixth day approached and evening came, they did not deviate in any way, neither to the right nor to the left, but there they spent the Sabbath and the Sunday. Even if it were a country of murder or of brigands, or even a land of wild animals, even if it were a land of death, even if it were a land of calamity and of sickness and of wrath, or a land of hunger and of thirst, and even if anything happened, there was no way for them to travel, but there they spent the Sabbath and the Sunday. They said: "Blessed are those who die because of the Word of God!".
ብሔር ። ወበእንተሂ ፡ መብልዕ ፣ ወመስቴ ፡፣ አልቦሙ ፡ ዘይገብሩ ፣ ወኢምንተኒ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፣ ዘአስተዳለውዎ ፡ በዕለተ ፣ ሳድስት ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕግ ። ወበሥርዓት ፣ ዘሐዋርያት ፣ ጽሑፍ ፣ ይብል ፡፣ ሐሙሰ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡
52 And also regarding food and drink, they did absolutely nothing except what they had prepared on the sixth day, according to what had been written in the law (Ex 16:22-30). And in the order of the Apostles there is the writing that says: "Complete your works in the five days; on the Sabbath and on the Sunday do no work". There is this and there are


[p. 200] {#po.51.p200}
ግበሩ ፣ ግብረክሙ ፡ በሰንበት ፣ ወበእሑድ ፣ ኢትግበሩ ፣ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ግብረ ፣ ዝኒ ፣ ወዘይመስሎ ፣ ሀሎ ፣ ብዙኅ ፡ ትእዛዘ ። ወበበዓላትኒ ፡ ቦዘይ ነግር ፡ ብዙኀ ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ ትእዛዝ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ይትመነደቡ ፡ በውስተ ፡፣ ፍኖት ፣ ተዘኪሮሙ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ፣ ሀብተ ፡ መካነ ፡ ዘይቴይስ ፡ እምውሉድ ፡ ወአዋልድ ፣ መንግሥተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ዘኢየኀልቅ ። ወዘበምድርኒ ፡ ዕቅበታተ ፡ ወበረከታተ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ነገር ፣ ተሰቅሉ ፡ ፈድፋደ ።
also many (other) regulations similar to it. And there are many things that it prescribes for the feasts. And they suffered many afflictions along the way because of this commandment, remembering the wealth of heaven, the place that is worth more than sons and daughters, the eternal kingdom that does not end. And (remembering) the treasures and the blessings that are obtained on earth by reason of this word, they were greatly elevated.

### The model is Ewostatewos
ወአባ ፣ ዐቢይ ፣ አቡነ ፡ ኤዎስጣቴዎስ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዐብየ” ፡ ወተለዐለ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡፣ ወኮነ ፡፣ ረኣዬ ፣ ኅቡአት ፡ ወፈዋሴ ፡ ድውያን ፡ ወሕ ሙማን ፡ ወእለሂ ፣ አጋንንት፤! ፡ አውፅአ ። ወዘ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ይከውኖ ። ወኩሉ ፣ ፍኖቱ ፣ ከመ ፣ ሐዋርያት ። ወበውስተ ፣ ፍኖቱሂ ፣ አግማል ፣ ሰገዱ ፡ ሎቱ ። ወቦ ፣ ብዙኃንኒ ፣ ዘገብረ ፡ ተአምረ ፣ ወኢን ክል ፣ ይእዜ ፣ ጽሒፎቶ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ዘይነግር ፡፣ ዘከመ ፣ ዐደወ ፡ ባሕረ ፣ ኢያሪኮ ፡ ነጺፎ ፡ ዐጽፎ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ማይ ፣ ከመዘ ፡ ሐመር ፡፣ ወዘከመ ፣ ቦአ ፣ አርማንያ ፡ ወበህየኒ ፡ ዘከመ ፣ አንሥአ ፣ ምውታነ ፣ ወፈወሰ ፣ ድውያነ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አዕረፈሂ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አውፅአ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዐፅፍ ፡ እማ ዕምቀ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ለዘ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ተዝካሮ ። ትምህርቱ ፣ ለኤዎስጣ”ቴዎስ" ፣ ስፉሕ ፣፡ ከመ ፡ መንጦላዕተ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ወፍሬሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ዘግባ ፡ ብዙኅ ። ኤዎስጣቴዎስ” ፣ ፈለገ ፣ ጥበብ ፣ ዘውስቴቱ ፣ ማይ ፡ ንጽሕ ፣ ኩሉ ፣ ዘከረዮ ፣ ይረዊ ፣ እምኔሁ ። ወዝኬ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ዘተስእለ ፣ ኪያሁ ፣ ይጠብብ ፣ በኀቤሁ ፡ ብሂል ። ኤዎስጣቴዎስ” ፣ ተከዜ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ነቅዐ ፡ ልቡና ። ኤዎስጣቴዎስ ፣ መዝገበ ፡ ጥበብ ፣ ምርካበ ፣ ተምኔታት ፡ ወኩሉ ፣ ዘይትፈቶ ። አይቴ ፣ ወአይቴ ፡ ዘኢተሰምዓ ፡ ትምህርትከ ፣ በውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምድረ ፣ ኢትዮጵያ ።
53 And the great father, our father Ewostatewos, became great for this work and was greatly exalted, and became a seer of hidden things, and a healer of the sick and infirm, and he cast out demons. And everything that he uttered with his voice was fulfilled for him. And every way of his was like that of the apostles. And along his way, the camels prostrated themselves before him. And there are also many (other) miracles that he did, but now we cannot write them; they are, however, in the book of his struggle which tells about how he crossed the sea of Iyyarikko, spreading his tunic over the waters as if it were a boat, and about how he arrived in Armenia and how there he raised the dead and healed the sick. 54 And also how, after his death, with that same tunic he extracted from the abyss of the sea him who would celebrate his memory. The teaching of Ewostatewos is extended like a tent of pure gold and its fruits are abundant like (those) of the cedar tree (Ps 91:13). Ewostatewos is a river of wisdom, inside which there is pure water; whoever digs it will be satisfied by it. And this means that whoever implores him will be wise in him. Ewostatewos is a river of knowledge, a source of intelligence. Ewostatewos is a storehouse of wisdom, he through whom aspirations and everything one desires are obtained. Where and where, in all the land of Ethiopia, has your teaching not been heard?


[p. 202] {#po.51.p202}
ወአነሂ ፡ ተነደፍኩ ፡ በፍቅርከ ፣ ከመ ፣ ዘይሜሥዕ" ፣ ርኀብ ፣ ይብሳዕ ፡ እክለ ፣ ከማሁ ፣ አኀዘኒ ፡ ሥሣዔከኻ ። ወከመ ፡ ዘይፈቱ ፣ ጽሙእ ፣ ይስ ተይ ፣ ማየ ፣ ከማሁ ፡ ጸምአት ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ብከ ። ኦአቡየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አንተ ፡ አሜሃ ፡፣ ወአነ” ፣ ዮም ፣ ናሁ ፡ በአዕይንተ ፡ ልብየ ፡ እሬኢ ፡ ፍናዊከ ። ከመ ፣ ትሴፎ ፡ ጽምእት ፣ ምድር ፣ ዝናመ ፡ ከማሁ ፡፣ እሴፎ ፣ ይረድ ፡ ዲቤየ ፣ በረከትከ ።
55 And I too have been wounded by your love (Ct 2:5): like the hungry man who longs to eat food, so the longing for you has seized me. And like a thirsty man desires to drink water, so my soul thirsts for you. O my father, as you (were) in that time, behold! I today with the eyes of my heart contemplate your ways. Like the thirsty land hopes for rain, so I hope that your blessing may descend upon me.

### Date of the death of Ewostatewos
ወኮነ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ኤዎስጣቴዎስ" ፣ አመ ፡፣ ፲ወድ ፣ ለሣህለ ፡ ኤሎል ፣ በሠርቀ ፡ አርማንያ ፣ ወበሐሳበ ፣ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወ2ቿ ፡ ለመስከረም ፡፣ በሰላመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አሜን ።
56 And the rest of Abba Ewostatewos occurred on the fifteenth in the clemency of Elol in the new moon of Armenia, and according to the computation of Ethiopia, on the eighteenth of Maskaram in the peace of God, Amen. Fiqator on the way to the court (first version).
ወእምዝ' ፣ ፳ ፡ ዕለተ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ውስተ ፣ ምድረ ፣ ቅድ ፡ ወሀለዉ ፣ አኃዊሁ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወባዕዳንሂ ፣ ብዙኃን ፣ መነኮሳት ፣ ወመስየ ፣ ሎሙ ፡፣ በህየ ፣ ዕለተ ፣ ሳድስት ፣ ወኀደሩ ፡ ህየ ። ወሶበ ፣ ጸብሐ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሳብዕት ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ እሉ ፡ ካልአን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወሖሩ ፣ ፍኖቶሙ ፡ እስመ ፣ ምድረ ፡ ቀትል ፡ ወሞት ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ምድር ። ወብፁዕሰ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ ወእሊአሁ ፣ ኢተግሕሠ ፡ እምህየ ፡፣ ኢለየማን ፡ ወኢ ለፀጋም ፣ በእንተ ፣ ሕግ ፣ ወሥርዓት ፣ ወአልቦሙ ፣ ኅብስት ፣ ዘይበልዑ ፡ ወኢማይ ፣ ዘይሰትዩ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ይሴፈዉ ፣ መዊተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ። ወብ”ፁዕሰ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ አውተረ ፣ ጸልዮ ፣ በመዝሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፣ ጥዑም ፡ ለጉርዓየ ፣ ነቢብከ ። 'እም ፡ መዐር“ ፡ ወሦከር ፣
57 After that, one day while the saint Fiqator was on the road in the territory of Qad and his brothers were with him and many other monks, evening came for them there on the sixth day, and they spent the night in that place. And when the seventh day dawned, those other men rose and set out on their way because this region was a region of brigandage and of death. But the blessed Fiqator and his (companions) did not deviate from that place, neither to the right nor to the left, because of the law and the order, and they had no bread to eat, nor water to drink, but they hoped to die for the Word of God. But as for the blessed Fiqator, he prolonged the prayer with the Psalms of David, saying: "Your speech is sweet to my throat, it is more delicious than honey and the honeycomb to my mouth" (Ps 118:103).
ጥዕመኒ ፣ ለአፉየ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፣ ለእሊአሁ ፣ ኢትፍርሁ ፡ አኃዊየ ፣ ብነ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ በሰማያት ፡ ዘኢይበሊ ፡ ወኢይማሰን ፡ ዘዐይን ፡ ኢርእየ ፡ ወእዝን ፡ ኢሰምዐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፣ ሰብእ ፣ ዘኢተሐለየ ። ተዘከሩ ፣ ፍቁራንየ ፡፣ ዘይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡፣ ወአንሰ ፣ በጽድቅከ ፣ እሬኢ ፡ ገጸከ ። ወእጸግብ ፡፣ በርእየ ፡ ስብሐቲከ ። ወመድኀኒነሂ ፣ አለበወነ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፣ ብፁዓን ፡ እለ ፡ ይርኅቡ ፣ ወይ ጸምኡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፣ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይጸግቡ ። ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ወዘይመስሎ ፣ ነገሮሙ ፣ ለእሊአሁ ፣ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ በቃለ ፣ መጻሕፍት ።
58 And he said to his (companions): "Do not fear, O my brothers! We have in heaven a hope that does not grow old and does not corrupt, which the eye did not see and the ear has not heard, which has not been thought of in the heart of man (Jer 3:16; 1 Cor 2:9). My beloved! Remember what the Prophet said: 'But I, in your justice, will contemplate your face and will be satisfied in contemplating your glory' (Ps 16:15). And our Savior also sobered us saying: 'Blessed are those who hunger and thirst regarding justice, because they will be satisfied' (Mt 5:6)". He said this and things similar to this to his (companions), and they rejoiced at the Word of the Books.

### Healing of a seriously ill man

59 And after the two Sabbaths had passed, they set out on their way and arrived in the land of Bawi of Angot. And they entered the house of a man whose name was Sala-Anbasa; that man was ill and had approached death. And his family members welcomed them according to the custom of hospitality and did good to them. And that man said to the blessed Fiqator: "O father! How would I not have thanked God if He raised me from this illness of mine, so that I might eat food and drink water". And the saint answered him and said to him: "Certainly you will eat food and certainly you will drink water; only believe in God, that He can do everything, because behold, He will forgive you and you will be healed of your illness. But you observe His commandment and venerate the Sabbaths and the day of the feasts in every time. And make the memory of Abba Ewostatewos and of Abba Absadi, our fathers, because they will intercede for you before the Lord". And after this he blessed him and passed on. After that, that sick man was healed by the prayer of the saint Fiqator and thanked God and said: "When I was in the torment of the illness, the apostle Peter came to me, and that guest, Abba Fiqator, was with him, and he said to him: 'Expel the illness from this man!'. And this Abba Fiqator drove the illness from the black appearance that was inside my flesh and indeed marked me with his right hand, and behold, now I am healed". And having heard this, his family members rejoiced at this fact and thanked God. Virtue of Fiqator.
ግዚአብሔር ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ አልቦ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ትካዘ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አርትዖ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወዐቂበ ፣ ትእዛዝ ፡፣ ዘመጻሕፍት ። ወእምዝ" ፡ አሐደ ፡ መዓልት= ፣ እንዘ ፣ የሐልፍ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ መንገለ ፡ ገራህት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ርኀብ ፡ ውእቱ ፣ ወጥጉዕ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ ርእየ ፡፣ ሠዊተ ፣ ዘውስተ ፡፣ ገራህት ፡ ወፈተወ ፡ ይብላዕ ። ወተዘከረ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ አመ ፣ ርኅበ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ በልዐ ፣ ሐባውዘ ፣ ርባን ፣ በቤተ ፣ አብያታር ፡ ካህን ፡ ወኢኮኖ ፡ ኀጢ አተ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ መሐዉ ፡ ሠዊተ" ፣ አርድእተ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ርኅቡ ፣ ወበልዑ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፣ ዚአሆሙ ፣ ወኢኮኖሙ ፡፣ ጌጋየ ። ወዘ ንተ ፡ ለቢዎ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እብላዕኑ ፡ እንጋ ፡ እስመ ፡ ርኅብኩ ፣ ወኢይከውነኒ ፡
60 But this saint Fiqator had no other concern except to rectify the faith and observe the commandments of the Books. After that, one (day) while this saint was passing toward the fields, and he was hungry and his stomach was stuck, he saw the ears of grain in the field and desired to eat. And he remembered what David did when he was hungry, about how he ate the loaves of the oblation in the house of Abiathar the priest and it was not (imputed as) sin to him (1 Sam 21:2-7). And again he thought about how the disciples of the Lord plucked the ears of grain when they were hungry and ate what did not belong to them and no error was (imputed) to them (Mt 12:1-8//). And having treasured this, he said: "Should I then eat because I am hungry, and will it not become sin for me?". And again he thought in his heart and said: "This action will become

ኀጢአተ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ በልቡ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ይከውነኒ ፣ ፍትወተ ፣ ከመ ፣ እንሣእ ፡ በእዴየ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡ መባሕት ። አኮኑ ፣ ጽሑፍ ፡ ዘይብል ፣ ኢትፍቱ ፡ ንዋየ ፣ ካልእከ ፣ ኢገራህቶ ፣ ወኢዘአጥረየ ፡ አጥርዮ ፡ ቢጽከ ። ወበካልእኒ ፣ ይብል ፣ ኃፍረት ፡ ነሚአ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ባዕድ ። ወዘንተ ፣ ለቢዎ ፣ ኢያንሥአ ፣ እዴሁ ፣ ከመ ፡፣ ይንሣእ ፣ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ አላ ፡ ኀለፈ ፡ ወሖረ ፣ እንዘ ፡፣ ርብ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሕገ ፣ እግዚአ ብሔር ።
for me a concupiscence if I take with my hand without authorization. Is there not the writing that says: 'Do not desire the property of your neighbor, nor his field, nor anything that your neighbor may possess' (Ex 20:17; Dt 5:21). And in another (passage) it says: 'It is a shame to take the property of others'". Having understood this, he did not (ever) raise his hand to take what was not his, but because of the law of God, he passed and went on even though he was hungry.

### Prediction of the future office of Fiqator. Again on his virtues
ወእምዝ ፣ ካዕበ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ጸውዖ ፣ ለቅዱስ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅ ጦር ፣ መነኮስ ፣ ኅሩይ ፣ ዘስሙ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ክርስ ቲያን ፣ ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፣ ብፁዕ ፡ ፊቅጦር ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ርኢ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ እስመ ፣ ሊተሰ ፡ ዝየ ፣ መቃብርየ ፡ ይከውን ። ወአንተሂ ፣ ሀለወከ ፡ ትንበር ፡ ዲበ ፡፣ መንበረ ፡ አበዊከ ፡፣ ቅዱሳን ፣ እምድ ኅረ ፡ ሞተ ፡ አባ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ መድኅን ፡ ወትከውን ፡ አበ ፣ ለብዙኃን ፣ እም ድኅሬሁ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ ኅቡአ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፣ ይኩን ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ብፁዕ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ ኮነ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተ ልእኮሙ ፣ ለቅዱሳን ፡ በንጹሕ ፡ ልብ ። ወበእንተ ፣ ሙቁሐን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይት ፈትሑ ። ወበእንተ ፣ ሕዙናን ፡ ከመ ፣ ይትናዘዙ ። ወበእንተ ፡ ግፉዓንሂ ፡ ወምንዱባን ፣ ከመ ፡ ይትባልሑ ። ወበእንተ ፣ መኳንንት ፣ ወሥዩማን ፡ እለ ፡ ርሕቁ ፣ በፍርሃተ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይዕርቆሙ ። ወበእንተ ፣ ዒውዋን ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ያግብኦሙ ። ወበእንተ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርታዕ ፡ ለዝ ፣ ግብር ፡ ኮነ ፡ መልእክቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ።
61 After that, again, one day the saint our father Fiqator called an elected monk whose name was Peter, while he was in church, and the blessed Fiqator came to him. And the old man said to him: "My son! Look at this land, because as for me, here will be my tomb. And you will certainly have to sit on the chair of your holy fathers, after the death of Abba Tawalda-Madahn, and after him, you will become the father of a multitude". The old man said this to him, because he saw the occult that was to happen (later). And indeed this blessed Fiqator became a lover of God and served the saints with a pure heart. And regarding the prisoners, so that they might be freed. And regarding the afflicted, so that they might be consoled. And also regarding the oppressed and the suffering, so that they might be saved. And regarding the princes and the officials who had moved away for fear of the king, so that he might reconcile them. And regarding the captured, so that he might return them (home). And regarding the faith, so that it might become right. His service was daily turned to this work. Fiqator meets the king.
ወብፁዕ ፣ ዘርአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ርቱዐ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፡ ፀንሐ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይሴፎ ፡ ምጽአቶሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተራትዕ ፣ ሃይማኖተ ፡ ወይሔሊ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ ዘከመ ፣ እፎ ፣ ይሠርዕ ፣ ሕገ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፣ አምላኩ ። ለቢዎ ፣ ቃለ ፡፣ መጽሐፍ ፡ ዘይቤ ። ኖን ፣ ማኅቶት ፡ ለእገርየ ፣ ሕግከ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ለፍኖትየ ። ወጠየቀ ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ነገረ ፣ ጳውሎስ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሐዋዝ ፣ ሕገ ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ይቀንዮ ፡ ለሰብእ ፣ አምጣነ ፡ ሕያው ፡ ውእቱ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ነገር ፡ አልዐለ ፡ ሕሊና ፣ ልቡ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይሥራዕ ፣ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ እንተ ፣ ሐዋርያት ። ወእምዝ ፣ በጽሑ ፡፣ ውስተ ፣ ዐፀደ ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ አባ ፡ ፊቅ
62 And the blessed Zar'a Ya'aqob, of the right faith, awaited with hope the coming of the saints to rectify the faith, and he thought much about how to arrange the law for the love of his God, because he had understood the word of the Book that says: "Nun. A lamp for my foot is your law, a light for my way" (Ps 118:105). And he understood again the word of Paul that says: "The law of God is sweet inside the heart of man, because the law submits man as long as he is alive" (Rom 7:22, 1). And by reason of this word, he elevated the thought of his heart so that he might administer Ethiopia through one (single) faith of the Apostles. After that, Abba Fiqator and his brothers arrived in the atrium of his court.
ጦር ፡ ወአኃዊሁ ። ወንጉሥሰ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ሀሎ ፣ አፍአ ፡ ተጺዕኖ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፈረስ ፡፣ ቀዬሕ ፣ ወኩሎሙ ፡፣ ሰራዊቱ ፡ ወትርሲተ ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ በየማኑ ፡ ወበፀጋሙ ፣ እንተ ፡ ድኅሬሁ ፡ ወእንተ ፣ ቅድሜሁ ። ወእምዝ ፣ አንሥአ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡
