ወርእዮሙ ፡፣ ለእሉ ፣ ቅዱሳን" ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይቀውሙ ፣ መንጸሮ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ሎሙ ፡፣ ጓእከ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡፣ አንትሙአ ፣ እምአይቴአ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፣ ቃለ ፣ ላእክ ፣ ውእቶሙኒ ፣ አውሥኡ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብሉ ፡ ንሕነአ ፣ ደቂቀ ፣ መዐቅባ ፣ እግዚእ ፣ እለአ ፣ መጻእነ ፣ እምደብረ ፣ ማርያምአ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖትአ ። ወሰሚዖ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወይቤ ፣ አቅርብ ዎሙ ፣ ኅቤየ ፣ ወቀሪቦሙ ፣ ኅቤሁ ፡ ሰገዱ ፣ ሎቱ ፡ ወአምኅዎ ፡ በኃለ ፣ ሰላም ፣ ወለሊሁኒ ፣ አምኖሙ ፣ በፍቅር ፡ ወተስእሎሙ ፡፣ በእንተ ፡፣ ዳኅና ።
they were standing in front of him. And he sent a messenger to them saying: "And you, whence (do you come)?". And when they heard the word of the messenger, they also answered saying: "We indeed are children of Ma'aqaba Egzi'e, those who have come from Dabra Maryam, because of the faith". Having heard (this), the king rejoiced greatly and said: "Make them approach me!". Having approached him, they prostrated themselves before him and greeted him with a word of peace, and he also greeted them with love and asked them about their safety.

### The question of Mount Zion
ወካዕበ ፣ አውሥአ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ትብሉ ፡ በእንተ ፡፣ ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ይእዜኑ ፣ ወሚመ ፣ ዘይመጽእ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፊቅ ጦር ፣ ወይቤሎ ፡፣ ንሕነሰ ፡ በከመ ፡፣ መሀሩነ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ለቢዎሙ ፣ መጻሕ ፍተ ፡ ነቢያት ፣ ወሐዋርያት ፣ መንግሥተ ፡፣ ደብረ ፣ ጽዮንሰ ፡ ተስፋሆሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ዘይከውን ፣ በተፍጻሜተ ፣ ፳፻ ፣ ዓመት” ። በከመ ፡ አሰ ፈወ“ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ በመጻሕፍቲሁ ። ወእለሰ ፣ ይበውእዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፣ መንግሥት ፡ ኅሩያን ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፣ ንጹሓን ፣ እምኃጢአት ። ወበእንተ ፣ ደብረ ፣ ጽዮንሰ ፣ ነገሩነ ፣ በመጻሕፍቲሆሙ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ነቢያት ፡ እለ ፣ ዳዊት ፣ ወኢሳይያስ ፣ ወዕዝራ ፣ ወዳንኤል ፣ ወባዕዳንሂ ። ወሐዋርያት ፡ አበዊነ ፣ እለ ፡፣ ጴጥሮስ ፡፣ ወጳውሎስ ፣ ወዮሐንስ ፣ ወአኃዊሆሙ ። ወበወን ጌልሂ ፣ ተነግረ ። ወሰሚዖ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ጣዕመ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ አንከረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፣ ወተአመኖ" ፣ በኩሉ ፡ ልቡ ። ወለዝንቱ ፣ አባ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ምስለ ፡ አኃዊሁ ፣ ኀረዮሙ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ፈድ ፋደ ፣ እምኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ማኅደሮሙ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ነዳያን ፣ እሙንቱ ፣ በእንተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ።
64 And again the king answered and said to them: "What do you say about Mount Zion (Rev 14:1)? Is it now or must it still come?". And the saint Fiqator answered and said: "We certainly, as our fathers who understood the books of the Prophets and of the Apostles have taught us, (maintain that) the kingdom of Mount Zion, the hope of the saints, will take place at the end of the seven thousand years (Dn 9:24ff). According to what God has promised in His books. And those who will enter that kingdom are all the elect, those who are free from sin (Rev 21:6-8). And about Mount Zion, the Prophets have indeed spoken to us in their holy books, such as David (e.g., Ps 47; 77:68), Isaiah (Is 2:2-3; 25; 60), Ezra (Esd 3:1-3), Daniel (Dn 9), and others still. And our fathers the Apostles, such as Peter, Paul, John, and their brothers. And it has been announced also in the gospel". And the king, having heard the goodness of the word of the saint Fiqator, was greatly admired and rejoiced for him and had confidence in him with all his heart. And the king chose this Abba Fiqator together with his brothers much more than all men and established their dwelling near him because they were poor for God. Digression: panegyric of Fiqator.
ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ብፁዕ ፣ ወቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ በቁለ ፡ ወልህቀ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ እሉ ፡፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትክልት ፡ ኀበ ፣ ሙሐዘ ፡ ማይ ፣ እንተ ፡ ትሁብ ፡ ፍሬሃ ፡ በበ ፡ ጊዜሃ ። ወከመ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ቄድሮስ ፡፣ በውስተ ፡ ሊባኖስ ፣ ተለዓለ ። ወከመ ፡ ፅፀ ፣ ቄጵሮስ ፣ በውስተ ፣ አድባረ ፡ ኤርሞን ። ወከመ ፡ ጽጌ ፡ ረዳ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡፣ ኢያሪኮ ። ወከመ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ዘይት ፡ ሠኖይ= ፣ ዘውስተ ፣ ገዳም ፣ ወጥዕመ ፣ መዐዛሁ ፣ ከመ ፣ ቀናንሞስ ፣ ወመ ልአ ፣ ኩመ ፣ ተከዜ ፣ ፊሶን ፡ ጥበበ” ። ወከመ ፣ ተከዜ ፣ ጤግሪስ ፡ በአ ውራኀ ፣ ኔዎን ። ወመልአ ፡ ምክረ” ፣ ከመ ፣ ተከዜ ፣ አፍራጢስ ፡፣ ወከመ ፣ ተከዜ ፣ ዮርዳኖስ ፣ በመዋዕለ ፡ ማእረር ። ወኮነ ፣ ባሕታዌ ፡ ወፅምወ” ፣ እምትካዘ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወአንደየ ፣ ርእሶ ፡ እምግብራቲሁ ፣ ለምድራዊ ፣
65 And this blessed and holy Abba Fiqator sprouted and grew in the midst of those saints like a tree planted by streams of water that gives its fruit in its time (Ps 1:3). "And like the cedar tree in Lebanon it has risen. And like the cypress tree on the mountains of Hermon. And like the rose in Jericho. And like a good olive tree in the desert, and its scent was sweet like cinnamon" (Sir 24:17, 18b, 19a, 20) and he was full of wisdom like the river Pishon. And like the river Tigris in the months of Newon (Sir 24:35). And he was full of counsel like the river Euphrates and like the river Jordan in the days of the harvest (Sir 24:36; Jos 3:15). And he became solitary and tranquil from the concern of this world and impoverished himself from the works of the earthly (life). But all his way and the thought of his heart was (turned) to the listening of the word of the


[p. 210] {#po.51.p210}
ቅኔት“ ። አላ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ ፍኖቱ ፣ ወሕሊና ፡፣ ልቡ ፡ አጽምኦ ፡ ቃለ ፡ መጻሕ ፍት ፣ ወገቢሮቶሙ፤ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ መጽሐፍ ፡ አፅምኡ"' ፣ ሕዝብየ ፡ ሕግየ ። ወጽልው ፣ እዝነክሙ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አፉየ ። ወሐዋርያ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ኩኑ ፣ ገባርያኒሃ ፡ ለሕግ ፣ ወአኮ ፡ ለአፅምኦ” ። ወጳውሎስኒ ፣ ይቤ ፣ ቦኑ ፡ በአፅምኦ” ፡፣ መጻሕፍት ፡ ይጸድቁ ፣ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አኮኑ ፡
books and to their implementation, as the Book says: "Listen, my people, to my law, and incline your ear to the word of my mouth" (Ps 77:1). And the apostle James says: "Be doers of the law and not (only) listeners" (Jas 1:22). And Paul also says: "Will they perhaps be justified before God by the listening of the books, will they not perhaps be justified by having put them into practice?" (Rom 2:13).
ዳእሙ ፣ በገቢሮቱ ፣ ይጸድቁ ። ዘንተ ፣ ወዘይመስሎ ፡ ለበወ ፡ ወተሐደሰ ፣ ልቡ ፣ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ወአውፅአ ፡ እምላዕሌሁ ፣ ሕሊና ፣ ዝ ፣ ዓለም ፡ ዘክቡድ ፡ እምእብነ ፡ መከራ" ፣ ለዘ ፣ ይጸውሮ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ሕዝቅኤል ፡ ነቢይ ፡፣ ግበሩ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ ልበ ፡ ሐዲሰ ፣ ወመንፈሰ ፣ ሐዲሰ ፡ ወአውፅኡ ፣ እምላዕሌክሙ ፣ ልበ"፤ ፣ እብን ። ወልሳነ ፣ ዕፍረትሂ ፣ ዘእምአርድእት ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኅድግዎ ፡ ለብሉይ ፡ ብእሲ ፣ ምስለ ፣ ምግባሩ ፣ ወልበስዎ ፡፣ ለሐዲስ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘአ ልቦ ፣ በኀቤሁ ፣ አይሁዳዊ”፤ ፡ ወኢአረማዊ” ። ኮነ ፡ መሥተዐግሠ ፣ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ተዐገሱ ፡ ወአጽንዑ ፣ ልበክሙ ። ኩልክሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ተወከልክሙ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ። አንደየ ፣ ርእሶ ፣ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ብፁዓን ፣ ነዳያን ፣ በመ ንፈስ ፣ እስመ ፡፣ ሎሙ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። አፍቀረ ፡ ገቢረ ፣ ጽድቅ ። እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ተመሀሩ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ እለ ፡ ትነ
66 He understood this and what is similar to it, and his heart was renewed by the Holy Spirit (Eph 4:23-24) and he removed from him the thought of this world which is heavier than the stone of testing (Sir 6:22) for him who takes it upon himself. As the prophet Ezekiel says: "Make for yourselves a new heart and a new spirit and take away from you the heart of stone" (Ez 18:31; 36:26). And also he who among the disciples is the fragrant speech has said: "Abandon the old man with his conduct and clothe yourselves with the new man in whom there is no Jew and pagan" (Col 3:9, 10, 11; Eph 4:22, 24). He became patient because it says: "Be patient and make your hearts firm, all you who have trusted in God" (Ps 30:25). He made himself poor, because it says: "Blessed are the poor in spirit, because theirs is the kingdom of heaven" (Mt 5:3). He loved to perform justice, because the prophet says: "Learn to do justice, you who inhabit the earth" (Is 26:9).
ብሩ ፣ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ። ወኮነ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ተፋቅሮ ፡ ፍጹም ፣ ሕግ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወኩሉ ፣ ፍኖቱ ፡ ወግዕዙ ፣ ወሑረቱ ፣ ወንብረቱ ፡ ወተን ሥኦቱ ፡ ወግብአቱ ፡ በፈሪሃ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። በከመ ፣ ጽሑፍ ፡ ዘይብል ፡፣ ብፁዕ ፣ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይፈርሆ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዘይፈቅድ ፡ ፍኖቶ ፡፣ ፈድ ፋደ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ብፁዓን ፣ እለ ፣ የኀሙ ፣ ስምዖ ። ወበኩሉ ፡ ልቦሙ ፣ የኀሥዎ ። ዘንተ ፣ ለቢዎ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ኀሣሜ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፣፡ ወትርጓሜሆሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ ጽሙደ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ለአንብቦ ፣ ውስተ ፣ መካነ ፡ ትምህርት ። ወድምፀ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ጥዑም ፡ ወአዳም ፡ ለሰሚዕ ፡ ወያነክሩ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፣ ሰምዕዎ ፡ ጥበቦ ፡ ወየውሀቶ ፣ ትሕትናሁ ፣ ወትዕግሥቶ ። ወምስለ ፡፣ ዘከመዝ ፣ ጊቲሩት ፡ ነበረ ፣ ብዙኀ ፣ መዋዕለ ፡ በረኀብ ፡ ወበጽምእ ። ወስቴሁኒ ፣ ወመብልዑኒ ፡ ረሰየ ፣ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወዝን ቱሰ ፣ አቡነ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ ይዌስክ ፡ እንተ ፡ ጽብሐት” ፣ ገቢረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወፍቅረ ፣ ሥላሴ ፡ ወምውቅ ፡ ልቡ ፣ በጸሎት ፡ ወኩሎ ፡፣ እንተ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ይሬስዕ ፣ ወዘቅድሜሁ” ፡ እሜልእ” ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዘድኅሬየ ፣ እሬስዕ ፡ ወዘቅድሜየ ፣ እሜልእ ፣ ወእዴግን ። ወአኀሥሥ ፡፣ ዕ
67 And he became a lover of men because the apostle said: "Love is a perfect law" (Rom 13:10). And every way and conduct of his, and his walking and his sitting, and his rising (Ps 138:2) and his entering (Zec 7:14) was in the fear of God. Like the writing that says: "Blessed is the man who fears God and who esteems his way much" (Ps 111:1). And it says again: "Blessed are those who seek his testimony and who seek him with all their heart" (Ps 118:2). Since he had understood this, he became a seeker of books and of their interpretation. And he committed himself to reading, every day, in the place of instruction. And the timbre of his voice was pleasant and sweet to listen to, and all those who listened to him admired his wisdom and his meekness, his humility and his patience. 68 And together with such goodness, he lived for many days in hunger and in thirst. And he indeed made the word of God his drink and his food. And this father of ours, Fiqator, every morning, increased the doing of good and the love of the Trinity, and his heart was warm for prayer and he forgot everything that he had done and pursued what was before him, as Paul says: "Because I forget what is behind me, and I pursue with what is


[p. 212] {#po.51.p212}
ትየ ፣ ከመ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዐይን ፡ ለጽዋዐ ፣ እምላዕሉ ፡ ዘእምኀበ ፣ እግዚአብ ሔር ።
before me and I chase. And I seek my reward like the sentinels, for the call from above by God" (Phil 3:14). Fiqator on the way to the court (second version).
ወእምዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፣ ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ መፍቀሬ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣ በጽሐ ፡፣ ኀበ ፣ ምድረ ፣ በድው ፣ ኀበ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ቤት"?' ፣ ዘየኅድሩ ፡፣ ወኢሰብእ” ፣ ዘየአምሩ ፡ ወቤተ ፡ ሆየ ፡ እስመ ፣ ኮነ ፡፣ ምሴተ ፡ ዐርብ ፣ እስመ ፣ ከማሁ ፣ ልማድ” ፣ ቦሙ ፡ ደቂቀ ፣ መዐቅባ ፣ እግዚእ ። ወምድሩሰ ፣ መፍርህ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወቀተልተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ይነብሩ ፣ ቦቱ ፣ ወእምዕፁብ”” ፣ ጥቀ ፡ የኀልፍዎ ፡ ነጋድያን ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ውኩል ፡ በኀይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አሰንበተ ፡ ህየ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ። ኢኀሠሠ ፣ ለከርሠ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ስንቅ፤ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፣ ኢረ ከበ ፣ ቤተ ፣ ኀበ ፣ ያሰምክ ፣ እስመ ፡፣ እንግዳ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡
69 After that, while he was on the road to go to the king who loved God, he arrived in a desert land in which there was no house in which they could spend the night, nor a person they knew. And there he spent the night, because it had become Friday evening. In fact, the children of Ma'aqaba Egzi'e had a similar custom. But the region was truly frightening and in it lived murderous people, and the merchants passed through it with the greatest difficulty. But this father of ours spent the Sabbaths there, according to what is written, trusting in the power of God. He did not seek (anything) to eat for his stomach, because he had (no) provisions with him; he did not find a house in which to lean (Mt 8:20), because he was a pilgrim, he wandered in the desert, he was crowned with the help of God.
ዔለ ፣ ረድኤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ተከለለ ። ወረከበ” ፣ ብእሴ ፣ መድኀኒተ ፣ ነፍሱ ፣ ዘይፈቱ ፡ ናዘዞ ፣ በቃሉ ፡ ወሴሰዮ ፣ እምእክለ ፡ ቤቱ ፣ ወኢያኅጥኦ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ቦቱ ። ናሁ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ በላዕሌሁ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ወአንትሙሰ ፣ መቅድመ ፡ ኅሠ ፣ መንግሥቶ ፡ ወጽድቆ ፡ ወዝንቱሰ ፣ ኩሉ ፣ ይትዌሰከክሙ ። ርእዩ ፡ አበውየ ፡ ወአኀውየ ፣ ኀደረ ፡ ውስተ ፣ በድው ፣ ወኢኀጥአ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ፣ እስመ ፣ እደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሴሰዮ ፣ ለዐቂበ ፣ ሕጉ ፣ ኀረዮ” ፣ በረከተ ፣ ዚአሁ ፣ አርአዮ ፣ ዘኢያእመረ ፣ ኅብስተ ፣ አወፈዮ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ ጽልመተ ፡ ወጐጻጉጸ ፡ ወኩሎ ፣ ፍኖተ ፣ መብእሰ ፣ ወይትፈለጡ ፡ እማእከሌነ" ፡ ፪ ፅደው ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተፈልጡ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ በርሀ ፡ ፍኖትነ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጎሐ ፣ ጽባሕ ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ተግኅሥ ፣ እምኔነ ፣ ይእተ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ወኮነ ፣ ኩሉ ፣ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ወረትዐት ፡ ፍኖትነ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘረከበነ ፡ ኅሱም ፡ እስመ ፣ ኢይትሐሰው ፡ ቃሉ ፣ ዘይከውን ፡ ይሬኢ ፣ ዘሀሎ ፡ ይትገበር ፣ ዘይ ደልዎ ፣ የአምር" ፣ ወበየውሀት ፡ የሐውር ፡ እስመ ፣ በኩሉ ፡ ይሴርሖ ፡፣
70 And he found a man who loved the salvation of his soul, he consoled him with his word and fed him with the food of his house, and he did not let him lack (anything) of what he had. Behold, what our Lord says in the gospel has been verified in him: "You then seek first His kingdom and His justice, while all this will be added to you" (Mt 6:33). Look, my fathers and my brothers, he dwelt in the desert and he did not lack food, because the hand of God fed him, He chose him to keep His law, He showed him His blessing, He gave him a gift of bread that he did not know (Ex 16:15). And after that he said: "I have seen the darkness and the roughness, and every harsh way" (4 Esd 5:12), "and the four men separated from us. And after those had separated, our way brightened and was like the dawn of the morning. And immediately they departed from us that day and everything happened according to what it says: 'And our road was leveled' and nothing evil has happened to us because His word does not become vain (Rom 9:6), He (fore)sees what must happen, what must occur, what must be known" (1 Cor 8:2). And he walked in meekness because in everything God accompanied him. Difficult journey, under the guidance of Providence.

71 After that, when he arrived in the region of Angot whose name was Yasaqa, it became Friday evening again and the Sabbaths arrived, and he spent the night in a desert area where there was neither tree nor water, except at a great distance. And the cold of that country was great and they did not have clothes that warmed, except goat skins (Heb 11:37), and they did not find fire that warmed.

ወብዙኅ ፡፣ ረ ፣ ብሔሩ ፣ ወአልቦሙ ፡ አልባሰ ፡፣ ዘያመውቅ ፡ ዘእን በለ ፣ ዘባድወ ፡ ጠሊ ፣ ወኢረከቡ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ዘያመውቅ ። ወፈድፋደሰ ፣ ኅጡ አን” ፣ እምስንቅ ፡፣ ወአልቦሙ ፡ ሲሲት”፤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኅዳጥ ፡ ማሴላ ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፣ ግብ ፣ ወሙሱን ፣ ጹናሁ ፡ ወርእየቱ ፡ ወውእቱኒ ፡ ዘኢተሐርጸ ፣ ወአልቦሙ ፣ መብልዕ” ፣ ዘእንበሌሁ ። ወለውእቱሰ ፡ ይወድይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወይለመልም ፡ ወበዕፁብ ፣ ግብር ፡፣ ይበልዕዎ ፣ ከመ ፣ ኢይሙቱ ፣ በረኃብ ፣ እስመ ፡ ተኩነኑ ፣ ለቃለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእንዘ ፣ ሀለዉ ፣ ካዕበ ፣ በዘከመዝ ፣ ረኃብ ፣ ወማይኒ ፡፣ አልቦሙ ፡፣ ዘይሰትዩ ፡ አላ ፡ ሶበ ፣ ይጸምኡ ፣ የሐውሩ ፣ ኅበ ፡ ፈለገ ፡፣ ማይ ፡ ወበህየ ፡ ይሰትዩ ፣ አስተብሪ ኮሙ ፣ በአፉሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፣ እንስሳ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ እስመ ፡ ሞቱ ፣ ለቃለ ፡፣ እግዚአብሔር ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወልደ ፣ ነጐድጓድ ፣ ዮሐንስ ፣ በራእዩ ፣ ወመጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ጸሐፍ ፡ ይእዜ ፣ ብፁዓን ፣ እለ ፣ ሞቱ ፡ በእንተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እወ ፣ እስመ ፡ ከማሁ ፣ ይቤ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፉ ፡ እምነ ፡ ጸማሆሙ ፡ ወተለዎሙ ፡፣ ግብሮሙ ፡ ወይወስዶሙ ፡፣ ኅበ ፡ ነቅዐ ፡ ማየ ፡ ሕይወት ። አማንኬ ፡ ደለዎ ፣ ለዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ትሩፍ ፣ ምሉአ ፣ መንፈስ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ በፍቅረ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ንዱፍ ፡ ሰትየ ፣ ማየ ፣ ሕይወት ፡ ወበልዐ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ሕይወት ፣
72 And even worse, they were deprived of supplies and had no food except for a little sorghum that was in a pit, which was bad both in its smell and in its appearance; moreover, it was not even ground, and they had no other food besides it. They would put it in water and it would become tender, and with great difficulty they ate it so as not to die of hunger, for they were submissive to the word of God. And while they were still in such hunger and did not even have water to drink, when they felt thirsty, they would go to the river and drink there with their mouths, kneeling like animals because of the Sabbath, for they were dead for the word of God. As the son of thunder, John, says in his vision: 'And a great voice came from heaven saying: "Write now! Blessed are those who have died for God, yes, for so says the Holy Spirit, that they will rest from their labor, and their deeds follow them and lead them to the spring of the water of life"' (Rev 14:13; 7:17). This father of ours, holy, perfect, filled with the Spirit, Fiqator, truly deserved this, struck by the love of God (Song 2:5), he drank the water of life and ate from the tree of life that is in the garden of God (Gen 2:8-14). Fiqator stops the storm three times.
ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነቱ ፣ ለአምላክ ። ወእንዘ ፣ ሀሎነ ፣ በይእቲ ፡ ምድር ፣ ምሴተ ፡ ዐርብ ፣ መጽኡ ፡ ዐበ ይት ፡ ደመናት ፣ ተጽዒኖሙ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወጸልመ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወወረዱ ፡ ዐበ ይት ፣ ዝናማት ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ ጥቃነ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ሀሎነ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ወተሀ ወክነ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እስመ ፡ አልብነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኖሰምክ ፡ ወኢኦመ ፡፣ ኀበ ፡ ንሴ ወር ፣ እምነፋስ ፣ ወእምቆባር ፡ ወእምዋፅየ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ናጸልል ። እስመ ፡ ምድረ ፣ በድው ፡ ውእቱ” ። ወንቤ ፣ በ፳ ቃል ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ እንዘ ፡፣ ንብል ፣ እግዚኦ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ገደፍከነ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ምድር ፣ እንተ ፣ አልባቲ ፡ ሰብእ” ፣ ወእንስሳ ፡ ወኢአብያተ” ። ወዘንተ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ንብል ፣ ተንሥአ ፣ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ትሩፍ ፡ ዘሐዋር ያት ፣ ዘውግ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ በትሮ ፡ ለኤዎስጣቴዎስ ፡፣ አቡነ ፡ ዘሀለወት ፡ ውስተ ፣ እዴሁ ፣ እስመ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ያፈቅራ ፣ ወይትአመና ፣ ወኢይፈልጣ ፡ እም ኔሁ ፣ እስመ ፡፣ ገባሪተ ፡ ተአምር ፡ ይእቲ ። ወተአምሪሃኒ ፡ ፅውቅ ፡ ወእ ምዝ ፣ አማዕተበ ፡ ባቲ ። ወይቤ ፣ አምሕለክሙ ፣ ኦመላእክተ ፡ ደመናት ፣ ወዝናማት ፣ ወመባርቅት ፡ ወነጐድጓድ ፡ በሥላሴ ፡ ወበእግዚእትነ ፣ ማርያም ፡ ወበጸሎቱ ፣ ለአቡነ ፡፣ ኤዎስጣቴዎስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢታውርዱ ፣ ዝናመ ፡ ኅበ ፡፣ ሀለውኩ ፡ አነ ፡ ምስለ ፣ አኃዊየ ።
73 And while we were in that land, on Friday evening great clouds came, heavy with rain, and the sky grew dark, and great rains descended and came very close to us, where we were, and we were greatly disturbed, because we had nowhere to take shelter, nor a tree to protect us from the wind and the darkness, nor anywhere to shelter from the heat of the sun. That was indeed a desert place. And all of us together said with one voice: 'Lord! Lord! Why have you abandoned us in this land that has neither men, nor animals, nor houses?'. And while we were saying this, our father Fiqator rose up, filled with the Holy Spirit, a companion of the Apostles, and took the staff of our father Ewostatewos that was in his hand, because he loved it very much and trusted in it and did not separate it from himself, for it was a worker of wonders. And his wonder-working was also known, and after that he made the sign (of the cross) with it. And he said: 'I adjure you, O angels of the clouds and of the rains, of the lightnings and of the thunders (Jub 2:2), by the Trinity and by our Lady Mary and by the prayer of our father Ewostatewos, that you do not let the rain fall where I am together with my brothers.'


[p. 216] {#po.51.p216}
ወዘንተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ተዘርዉ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ዝናማት ፡ እንዘ ፣ ንሬኢ ፡ በአዕይንቲነ ፣ ወአእኩትናሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ውለዕበየ ፡ ጸጋሁ ። ወእምዝ ፣ ካዕበ ፣ በሳኒታ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ዝናማት ፡ ኀበ ፣ ሀሎነ ፡ ቅሩባነ ፡ ወአቡነ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ገብረ ፣ በከመ ፣ ቀዳሚ ። ወዓዲ ፣ በሣልስታኒ ፣ መጽኡ ፣ ከማሁ፤ ፣ ደመናት ፡፣ ዘምስለ ፣ ግርማ ፡ ዐቢይ ። ወካዕበ ፣ አውየውነ ፣ ኅበ ፣ አምላክ ፣ ወተንሥአ ፣ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ወገብረ ፣ ከማሁ ፡ ካዕበ ፣ በከመ ፡ ያለምድ ፡ ወተአተ ፣ ኀይለ ፡ ዝናማት ፡ እምኔነ ፡ ወኮነ ፡፣ ዛሕነ ፡ ወተፈሣሕነ ፣ ወአዕረፍነ ፡ ወመርሐነ ፣ ውስተ ፡ መርሶ ፣ ኀበ ፣ ፈቀድነ” ፣ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ነቢይ ፣ ርእዩ ፣ ቀደምተ ፣ ዓለም ፡ ወአእምሩ ፣ መኑ ፡፣ ተአመነ ፣ በእግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወተኃፈረ ። ወመኑ ፡ ተወከለ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወገደፎ ፡ ወመኑ ፡ ጸውዖ ፣ ወተጸመሞ ፣፡ እስመ ፣ መሐሪ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ወመስተሣህል ፣ ወያድኅን ፡ በዕለተ ፣ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወይባልሕ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ ወተሰጥዎ ፡ ሰአሎ ፣ ወተሰናአዎ ፣ ተአመኖ ፣ ወአድኀኖ ፣ እምተሠርዎ ።
74 And when he said this, the rains dispersed immediately while we were watching with our own eyes, and we thanked God and the greatness of his grace. After that, the next day the rains came again near where we were, and our holy father did as he had the first time. And again, for the third time, in the same way, the clouds came along with great terror. And again we cried out to God, and our father Fiqator rose and did the same thing again, according to his custom, and the power of the rains moved away from us, and there was quiet, and we rejoiced and rested, and he led us to the port to which we wanted (to arrive), as the prophet says: 'Observe those who are (our) predecessors in the world and know: who has trusted in God and been ashamed? And who has hoped in him and been abandoned? And who has called upon him and he has been deaf to him? For he is merciful and compassionate, and in the day of affliction he saves and delivers' (Sir 2:10-11). And this father of ours, Fiqator, also called upon him and he answered him, he besought him and he complied with him, he trusted in him and he saved him from destruction.

### Rhymed Eulogy to Fiqetor
አማን ፣ አማን ፡ ለኤልያስ ፣ ተመሰሎ ፡፣ ለደመና ፣ አምሐሎ ፡፣ ከመ ፣ ኢይዝንም ፡ ወኢይክዐው ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ዘሀሎ ። ለይእቲ ፡ በትር ፣፡ ከመ ፣ በትረ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ እብላ ፡ ለፌ ፣ ወለፌ ፣ ኀይለ ፡ ዝናማት ፡፣ አሰሲላ ፣ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ዘረሰየት ፡ ተድላ ። ለመኑ ፣ ተውህቦ ፣ ዘከምዝ ፡ ግርማ ፣ ዘእንበለ ፡፣ ለፊቅጦር ፡ አቡነ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ተቅዋማ ። መፍቀሬ ፡ ጻማ ፡ ከመ ፡ በርሶማ ። ለመጽሐፈ ፡፣ ሕይወት ፡፣ ተፍእማ ፣ እንበለ ፣ አስትቶ ፡ ፈጸማ ፡ ንወድሶ ፡ በዜማ ። ለልብ ፣ ዘይኤድማ ፣ በመዋቅሕተ ፣ ቀኖና ፣ ለሥጋሁ ፡ አሕመማ ፣ ወበዕፍረተ ፣ መንፈስ ፣ ለነፍሱ ፣ ኀተማ” ፣ ለመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ጥዕማ ፣ ወተባደረ'” ፣ ከመ ፡ ይሠየማ ፣ ለከብካበ ፣ መርዓ ፣ ተሳለማ ፣ ወፈተወ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ስማ ፣ ለሥርወ ፣ ኑፉቃን ፡፣ ገዘማ ፣ ጉጐድበ ፣ መለኮት ፣ ነሚኦ ፣ እምራማ ። ለኢትዮጵያ ፣ ተፈሥሐት፤ ፡ አድያማ ። እምፄወ፤ ፣ ትምህርቱ ፣ ተቀሲማ' ፡ አባ ፣ አባ ፡ ንጽሐ ፡፣ አስኬማ ፣ ቃለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ዘተደረ ፡፣ ከመ ፣ ሄርማ ፣
75 Truly, truly, he resembled Elijah; he adjured the cloud so that it would not rain and would not pour down what was above it (1Kgs 18:44-46). Of that staff I say that it is like that of Moses: it removed the power of the rains from one side to the other (Ex 14:15-21), which in his hands brought joy. To whom was such authority ever given, if not to Fiqator our father, the candelabra of the church? A lover of labor like Barsauma. He swallowed the book of life (Rev 10:10) and fulfilled it without negligence; let us praise him with the song that pleases the heart. He made his flesh suffer with the chains of penance and anointed his soul with the ointment of the Spirit; he tasted the kingdom of heaven and competed to be set over it; he joined the wedding feast (Rev 19:9) and desired to take its name; he cut the root of the heretics, taking the axe of divinity from the height of the heavens; the borders of Ethiopia rejoiced, (being) seasoned with the salt of his doctrine. Abba! Abba! of the immaculate monastic habit, who dined on the word of God as


[p. 218] {#po.51.p218}
ዘተቀጸለ ፣ በንጽሑ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ቃማ ፣ በረከቱ ፣ ወበረከተ ፣ አበዊሁ ፣ ቅድመ ፡ ልዑል ፣ ቀዌዊማ ፣ ታስተምህር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለዘርአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ለፀቢአ ፣ ፀሩ ፣ አስተሐሚማ ፣ ታስተፍሥሖ ፣ ወትረ ፣ ዕልዋነ ፣ መንግሥቱ ፡ አስጢማ ። ወለነኒ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፣ ታድኅነነ ፡ እምክረ ፡ መስቴማ ፡ አሜን ። ወአሜን ። ።
Hermas, who for his purity was girded with the golden necklace, his blessing and the blessing of his fathers, standing before the Most High, intercede solicitously for him, for Zar'a Ya'aqob, for the defeat of his enemies, may he always gladden him, sinking those who rebel against his kingdom, and may he also preserve us, his children, from the counsel of Mastema, amen and amen.

### Zar'a Ya'aqob presides over the debate on Mount Sayon
ወበውእቱ ፣ መዋዕል ፣ ተንሥኡ ፣ መስተካሕዳን ፡፣ እለ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አልቦ ፣ ትንሣኤ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፣ በመዋዕለ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ዘርአ ፡ ያዕ ቆብ ፡ ሰይፍ ፡ በሊሕ ፣ በስመ ፣ ሥላሴ ፣ ዘይትሜካሕ ፣ ሥርወ ፣ ዕልዋን ፣ ዘይመልሕ ፣ ሦከ ፡ አበሳ ፣ ዘይጸይሕ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ዘይፈትሕ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ለአዝማን ፣ ነዋኅ ፣ ወለዓመታት ፣ ብዙኅ ፣ ጸላእቶ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይጸይሕ ፣ እሊአሁ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ያስተፌሥሕ ። ወሶበ ፣ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፣ አዘዘ ፣ ጉባኤ ፡ ዐቢየ ፣ ከመ ፣ ይትጋብኡ ፣
76 And in those days heretics arose who said that there is no resurrection of Mount Sayon, in the days of our king Zar'a Ya'aqob, a sharpened sword, who gloried in the name of the Trinity, who uprooted the root of the rebels, who removed the thorn of guilt, who judged with the word of justice—may the Lord give him to us for long ages and for numerous years, while he rids himself of his enemies and while he gladdens his own. And when he heard this word, he commanded that a great assembly be convened, so that the great ones of his kingdom, the priests and the high priests, the naburanas and the teachers and all the monks, the princes and the governors, the officers and the dignitaries and all the elders of the people might gather, and there was a great assembly. The king dictates the procedures for conducting the debate
ዐበይተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ካህናት ፣ ወሊቃነ ፡ ካህናት ፣ ንቡራነ ፡ እድ ፡ ወምም ህራን ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፣ መነኮሳት ፡ መሳፍንት ፡ ወመኳንንት ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወዐበ ይት ፣ ወኩሎሙ ፣ ሊቃውንተ ፡ ሕዝብ ፣ ወኮነ ፣ ጉባኤ ፡ ብዙኀ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በቃል ፣ ጥዑም ፡ ወበከናፍር ፣ ሥእሥአት?" ፣ ደብተራ ፡፣ ይፍትሑ ፣ ለክሙ ። ወተዋሥኡ ፣ በጅወ፳ ፣ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ሕግ ፣ ወተፋለጡ ፣ በበይናቲክሙ ፡ እለ ፣ ትብሉ ፡ ቦ ትንሣኤ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፣ ወእለሂ ፡ ትብሉ ፡ አልቦ ። ወፈለጦሙ ፡፣ ዘዘ ፡፣ ዚአሆሙ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፣ ይፈልጥ ፣ ኖላዊ ፣ አባግዐ ፣ እምአጣሊ ፡፣ እስመ ፣ እሙራን ፣ እሙንቱ ፡፣፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወደቂቀ ፡ ጋኔን ። ወእምዝ ፣ ቆሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡፣ ፊቅ ጦር ፡ ወአባ ፡ አምኃ ፡ ለጽዮን ፡ ዐቃቤ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ዘሐይቅ ፣ ምሉአነ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ዘወንጌል ፣ አርጋብ ። ወእለ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፣ ብዙኃን ፣ መነኮሳት ፡ ኮኑ ፣ እንተ ፣ በየማን ። ወመስተካሕዳንሰ ፣ ቆሙ ፡ እንተ ፡ በፀጋም ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ካሕደ ፡፣ ዐቢየ ።
77 And the king said to them with a gentle voice and with eloquent lips: 'Let the learned administer justice to you. And discuss based on the eighty-one books of the law and separate yourselves from one another; those who say that there is the resurrection of Mount Sayon and those who say that there is not.' And he separated them, each according to his (faction), as the shepherd divides the sheep from the goats (Mt 25:32); for the children of God and the children of the devil are recognizable. After that, our father Fiqetor and Abba Ammeha LaSayon, agabe sa'at of Hayq, filled with wisdom, doves of the gospel, stood up, and numerous monks who were with them stood on the right side. And as for the heretics, they stood on the left side, and there was a great contradiction.


[p. 220] {#po.51.p220}

### Bitter contradiction based on biblical texts
ወአውሥኡ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ የማን ፡ አባ ፡ አምኃ ፡ ለጽዮን ፡ ወአባ ፣ ፊቅ ጦር ፣ ወይቤልዎሙ ፡፣ ምንተኑ ፡ ትፈቅዱ ፡፣ ናምጽእ ፣ ለክሙ ፡ እመጻሕፍት ። ወኀረዩ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ከሐዲያን ፣ አቡ ፣ ቀለምሲስ" ፣ ወኀብሩ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ በዝ ፡ ነገር ። ወተርጐሙ ፣ እሙንቱ ፣ ከሐድዲያን ፡ በነገረ ፡ ሐሰት ፣ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ርቱዐ ፡ እለ ፣ ብዔል ፡ ዜቡል ፡ አንበቦሙ ። ይቤሉ ፡ ዘሀሎነ ፣ ዓለም ፣ ዝውእቱ ፣ መንግሥተ ፡፣ ደብረ ፡፣ ጽዮን ፡ ወምሳሕኒ'፤ ፡ ሥ።ጋ ፣ ወደም ። ወአውሥኡ ፣ ሰብአ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡፣ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ወልደ ፣ ነጐድጓድ ፡ ይትነሥኡ ፣ ምውታን ፣ ወይሠዐር ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወይከ ውኑ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፣ ካህናቲሁ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዓመታቲሁኒ" ፡ ፲፻ ፡ እማ ንቱ ። ወቦ ፣ ዓዲ ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፣ ሐሳዊያነ ፡ ወዘማዊያነ ፡ ዘዘ ፣ ዚአሁ ፣ ብዙኀ ፣ ኀጢአተ ። ኩሎሙኑ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ በእን ተዝ ፣ ተዐውቀ ፣ ሐሰትክሙ ። እፎ ፣ እንከ ፡ ታስተማስልዋ ፡ ለደብረ ፣ ጽዮን ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኃላፊ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፣ መዋቲያን ። ማእዜኑ ፣ ተንሥኡ ፣ ምውታን ፡ ወማእዜኑ ፡ ተአስረ ፡ ሰይ ጣን ። ነጽሩ ፣ እስኩ ፣ መንገለ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ካህናትኑ ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡፣ እስመ ፣ ካህናትሰ ፣ ኅዳጣን ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ይበዝጉ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡፣ ክህነት'' ። ወበምንት ፡ ትዌስክዎ" ፡ ለ፳፻ ፡ ትፈ ቅዱኑ ፣ ታጽርፅዎ ፡ ለቃለ ፡ ወልደ ፣ ነጐድጓድ ፣ በእንተዝ ፣ ኢንትሜጠዎ ፡፣
78 And from the people who were on the right, Abba Ammeha LaSayon and Abba Fiqetor discussed and said to them: 'What then do you wish that we bring to you from the books?' And those heretics chose the Apocalypse, and all joined in this word. And those heretics interpreted with a word of falsehood, which was not upright, which Ba'el Zebul (Lk 11:15) had made them utter. They said: 'The world in which we live, this is the kingdom of Mount Sayon, and the meal is indeed the Body and the Blood.' And the people of faith contradicted (them), saying: 'Does not the Son of Thunder say: "The dead will rise and Satan will be defeated and all will be priests of the Lord and his years will be a thousand" (cf. Rev 20:1-6)? And in this world there are also liars and fornicators and much sin of every kind. Are they perhaps all people of Mount Sayon? For this reason your lie has been manifested! How then do you compare Mount Sayon with this passing world if those who live in it are all mortal? When have the dead ever risen? And when has Satan ever been chained? Look, pray, toward the Christian people! Have they perhaps all become priests? Indeed, the priests are very few and the majority of the people do not have the priesthood. And with what will you add the five hundred (years)? Do you perhaps want to nullify the word of the Son of Thunder? For this reason we will not accept your word because you are raving in vain.'
ለነገርክሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ በከ ፡ ትዘነግዑ ። በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፣ ሐዋርያ ፡፣ ወአንትሙሰ ፡ አኃዊነ ፣ ተዘከሩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘነገ ሩክሙ ፣ ቀዲሙ ፡ ሐዋርያቲሁ ፡ ለኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ዘይቤሉክሙ ፡ ከመ ፣ በደኃሪ ፣ መዋዕል ፡፣ ይመጽኡ ፣ መስሕታን ፣ እለ ፡ ይምህሩ ፡ በፍትወተ ፣ ልቦሙ ፡ ወያስሕቱ ፡ በኃጣውኢሆሙ ። እሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ እለ ፡ ይስሕቡ ፣ በጌጋዮሙ ፣ ወያስሕቱ ፣ በኃጣውኢሆሙ ፣ ወመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ አልቦሙ ። እሉ ፣ እሙንቱ ፡ ተክለ ፣ መርገም ፡ እለ ፡ በቁሉ ፡ በዝናመ ፡ መንሱት ፡ ወተሰቅዩ"፤ ፡ በጠለ ፡ ሲኦል ። ወነፋሰ ፣ ሐሩር ፣ ነሥኦሙ ፣ ፍጡነ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ተገፍትኦቶሙ ። እስመ ፣ ሜጥዎ ፡ ለቃለ ፣ መጻሕፍት ፡ ውስተ ፣ ግዕዞሙ ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ ተንሥኡ ፡ በጥብዓት ፡ አባ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፣ ወአባ ፡ አምኃ ፡ ለጽ ዮን ፣ ወተዋሥእዎሙ ፣ ወሞእዎሙ ፡ በፍትሕ ፡ ወበርትዕ ። ወበዝኒ ፡ ኢኀ ደጉ ፣ እከዮሙ ፣ ወተቃወምዎሙ ፡፣ እንዘ" ፣ ይትገአዙ ፡ ክልኤተ ፣ ዕለተ ።
79 'As the apostle said: "And you, our brothers, remember the word that the apostles of Jesus Christ proclaimed to you at the beginning. That they told you that in the last days impostors will come who teach with the passion of their heart and who seduce with their sins" (cf. 2Pt 3:1-3). These are those who drag (others) with their error and who with their sins lead astray and who do not have the Holy Spirit. These are the plant of the curse that have sprouted with the rain of calamity and that have been watered with the dew of Sheol. And the wind of the heat has carried them away (Ps 1:4) and fast has been their overthrow. For they have turned the word of the Scriptures to their own way of behavior (2Pt 3:16).' And for this reason Abba Fiqetor and Abba Ammeha LeSayon rose with courage and contradicted them and defeated them with justice and righteousness. And not even with this did they abandon their malice, but they opposed them, refuting them for two days. The debate continues on the third day
ወበሣልስት ፡፣ ዕለት ፣ ጸውዖሙ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ወአቀሞሙ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወተጠየቀ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ። ወተሐየለ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀናኢ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወኢፈቀደ ፡ ያብጥል ፡ ተስፋ ፣ ቅዱሳን ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ አንሥአ ፡፣ ቃሎ ፡፣ ንጉሥ ፡፣ ዘርአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ አስተጋቢኦ ፡ ብሉየ ፡ ወሐዲሰ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጽጉበ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ውእቱ ። ወአውሥኦሙ ፡፣ በየውሆ ፡ ወአኮ ፡ በግርማ ፣ አላ ፣ ከመ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ዐርክ ፡
80 And on the third day, the king convened them all and made them stand before him, and the word of both (factions) was verified. And (Fiqetor) was fortified in the Holy Spirit, because he was jealous for the Christian faith and did not wish to make the hope of the saints vain. And immediately King Zar'a


[p. 222] {#po.51.p222}
ምስለ ፣ ዐርኩ ። ወከሐዲያንሰ ፣ ተናገሩ ፡ በከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፣ ወውእቱሰ ፣ ሞኦሙ ፡ በፍትሕ ፣ በዘ ፡ አምጽኡ ፣ ለሊሆሙ ፣ እስመ ፣ ጥበብ ፡ ኮነቶ ፡ እመ ፡ ወበአእምሮ ፡ ተሐፅነ ፡ ፍጹመ ፣ በፄወ ፣ መለኮት ፣ ተቀስመ ፣ ዲበ ፡ ኩኩሐ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ቆመ ። ውስተ ፣ ባሕረ ፣ መርገም ፡ ዕልዋነ ፡ አስጠመ ፣ ወበሰይፈ ፡ ግዘት ፣ አፉሆሙ ፣ ኀተመ"' ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይዕቀቦ ፡ እስከ ፣ ይመጽእ ፡፣ ዳግመ ፣ ዘአልቦ ፣ ሞት” ፣ ወኢሕማም” ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይኩንን ፡ ኩሎ ፡፣ ዓለመ ፡ አሜን ።
Ya'eqob raised his voice, invoking the Old and the New (Testament), because he was sated with Books. And he answered them with meekness and not by striking terror, but as a friend who speaks with his friend. And the heretics spoke as at the beginning, but he defeated them in justice, with those same (arguments) that they adduced, because wisdom had become a mother to him (4Esd 13:55), and he had been raised perfectly in knowledge, he had been seasoned with the salt of divinity, he had been raised upon the rock of faith. He sank the rebels in the sea of the curse and with the sword of excommunication closed their mouths—may the Lord preserve him until he comes for the second time, without death or passion, when he will judge all the world (Mt 25:31), amen! Spectacular intervention of Fiqetor
ወእምዝ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ከመ ፣ ያእትት ፡ ኑፋቄ ፡ እም ልበ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፣ መሀይምናን ። ወይቤሎ ፡፣ ለንጉሥ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ቃል ፣ አዝዝ ፣ ያንድዱ ፡ እሳተ ፣ ከመ ፡፣ ከላድዮን ፣ ወንባእ ፡፣ ውስቴቱ ፡ አነ ፡፣ ወዝንቱ ፡ ዐላዊ ፡ ወይፍታሕ ፣ ላዕሌነ ፡ እሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጠየቅ ፡ ትምህርተ ፣ ክርስቶስ ። ወኢያንጌግዩ ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ በማእከል ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ አዘዘ ፣ ንጉሥ ፣ ያንድዱ ፡ እሳተ ። ወአንደዱ ፣ ወተለዐለ ፡ ነበልባሉ ፡ እስመ ፣ ኀያል ፡ ቃለ ፣ ንጉሥ ።
81 After that, our father Fiqetor desired to remove the heresy from the heart of all the believers. And he said to the king in a loud voice: 'Order that they light a fire as at Kaladayon (Dan 3) and let me and this rebel enter it, and let the fire be our judge, so that the teaching of Christ may be ascertained and people may not go wandering here and there, in the midst (of this diatribe)!' And at that instant the king ordered that they light the fire, and they lit (it), and its flames rose up, because the word of the king is strong.

### Fiqetor submits to the ordeal by fire
ወእምዝ ፡ ሮጸ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ገዲፎ ፡ ልብሶ ፡ እስመ ፡ አምእ ስተ ፣ በግፅ ፣ ሕሱየ ፡ ይለብስ ፣ ጉየ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ውስተ ፣ እሳት ፣ እስከ ፡ ደንገፁ ፡ ኩሉ ፣ ጉቡአን ። ወለሊሁ ፣ ንጉሥኒ ፣ ተደመ ፡ ነጺሮ ፣ ጥብዐቶ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡፣ ከሐዲ ፡ ሑር” ፣ አንተሂ ፡ ባእ ፣ ምስሌሁ ። እስመ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ሃይማኖትከ ፡ ይቴይሰከ ፣ መዊተ ። ወአውሥአ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ አበስኩ ፣ እኔስሕ ፡ እስመ ፡ አዘዘ ፣ መድኀኒነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕ የው ፣ ኩሉ ፡ በንስሐ ። ወወድቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ርዑደ ፣፡ ወድ ንጉፀ ፣ ወከልሐ ፡ በዐቢይ ፣ ቃል ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፣ መሐረኒ ፣ ወእምዝ ፣ እትወገዝ ፡ በስመ ፣ ሥላሴ ፣ ከመ ፣ ኢይንብብ ፣ ወኢይምሀር ፡ ወከመ ፡ ኢይበል ፡፣ አልቦ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ድብረ ፡ ጽዮን ። ወበዝ ፡ ተወግዘ ፡ በአፉሁ ፣ በሰይፈ ፣ ሥላሴ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ በአብ ፣ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውጉዘ ፡ ለእኩን ። ወአቡነ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፣ አጥብዐ ፡ ለበዊእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዘአድኀኖሙ ፣ ለአናንያ ፣ ወለአዛርያ ፡ ወለሚሳኤል ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ያድኅነኒ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ለበዊእ ፡ እስከ ፣ ኀለፈ ፡ እሳት ፣ መልዕልተ ፣ ርእሱ ፡ ወከደኖ ፣ በነበልባሉ ፣ እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ እስከ ፡ ከደነ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ በጢሱ ።
82 And after this, our father Fiqetor ran, leaving his garments because he was wearing tanned sheepskins. He ran fast to enter the fire, to such an extent that all those gathered feared. And even the king himself was astonished at observing his courage. And he said to that heretic: 'Go! You too enter with him. Indeed it is better for you to die for your faith.' But (he) answered saying: 'I have sinned! I repent, because our Lord has commanded that everyone be saved through repentance.' And he fell to the ground and became trembling and frightened and cried out in a loud voice saying: 'Forgive me! And from now on I declare myself interdicted in the name of the Trinity, that I will not speak and will not teach and that I will not say that there is no resurrection of Mount Sayon!' And in this way he made himself interdicted with his (own) mouth with the sword of the Trinity when he said: 'May I be interdicted in the Father, in the Son and in the Holy Spirit!' But our father Fiqetor had the courage to enter (the fire), saying: 'He who saved Ananias, Azariah and Mishael, he will save me!' And he came to enter (the fire), so much so that the fire passed over his head and covered him with its flame, because there was an exceedingly great fire, so much so that it covered the sky with its smoke.


[p. 224] {#po.51.p224}

### The king stops Fiqetor's run toward the pyre
ወሶቤሃ ፣ አልዐለ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤ ፣ አኀዝዎ ፣ ፍጡነ ፡ ዘእን በለ ፣ ይባእ ፣ ውስተ ፣ እሳት ። ወሶቤሃ ፣ ሮጹ ፣ አግብርተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፣ ወአኀዝዎ ፣ ከመ ፣ ኢይባእ ፣ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ሰራዊቱ ፣ እስመ ፡ ኢተረክበ ፣ እምቀደምቱ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡፣ ዘከመዝ ፣ ግብር ።
83 And immediately the king raised his voice and said: 'Grab him quickly, before he enters the fire!' And the servants of the king ran immediately and grabbed him so that he would not enter (the fire), and the king and all his army rejoiced because in Ethiopia such a fact was not found among his predecessors.

### Merits of Zar'a Ya'aqob
እስመ ፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ረትዐ ፣ ሃይማኖተ ፡፣ ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ወኩሉ ፡፣ ሥርዓተ ፡ ሕግ ። ወሐደሰ ፡ ለነ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ሰንበታት ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዙ ፣ ሐዋርያት ፣ በሲኖዶሶሙ ። ወአስምዐ ፡ ዐዋዲ" ፣ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ ሐሙሰ ፣ መዋፅለ ፣ ግበሩ ፣ ግብረክሙ ፡ ሰንበተ ፡፣ ወእሑደ ፣ ኢትግበሩ ፡፣ ቦቶን ፣፡ እምግብር ፡ ኩሉ ፣ 'በዐላትዝ ፣ ዘተጽሕፈ” ፣ አዘዘ ፣ ከመ ፣ ያክብሩ፡ ። ወበዐላቲሃኒ ፣ ለእመ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ለነ ፣ ዘነአምር” ፡ ወዘኢነአምር”" ። ወሠርዐ ፣ ይግበሩ ፣ ተዝካራ ፣ በውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወኢየዐርፍ ፡፣ ለሰብሖታ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዐልተ ፣ እስመ ፣ ወይነ ፣ ፍቅራ ፡ አስከሮ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ወደሰ ፣ ክልኤ ፡ ድንግልናሃ ፣ በሕሊናሁ ፣ አስተናቢሮ ፣ እስመ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ለነቢብ ፣ አገበሮ ፣ ወጸገዎ ፡ ጥበበ ፡ ወአእምሮ ፣ ከመ ፣ ያብዝኅ ፣ ሰላመ ፣ እሙ ፣ ዘምስለ ፡ ዘምሮ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ያጽንዕ ፣ መንበሮ ፣ ወያስተባዝኅ ፣ ክብሮ ፡፣
84 Indeed, during his days the faith in (the doctrine of) Mount Sayon and all the provisions of the law were rectified. And he renewed for us the respect for the Sabbaths, according to what the Apostles commanded in their Synod. And the herald proclaimed saying: 'For five days do your work (but) on Saturday and Sunday do not do any work.' He ordered that they observe these feasts that had been prescribed. And he also revealed to us the feasts of the Mother of Life, (those) that we know and (those) that we do not know. And he arranged that they make her memory in the whole region, and he did not take rest from praising her night and day because the wine of her love had intoxicated him. And for this reason he praised her double virginity, placing it in his thought. Indeed the Spirit of his Son had induced him to speak and had given him wisdom and knowledge so that he might multiply the peace of his mother together with the song. May he strengthen his throne for ages of ages and increase his glory, and above all may he protect him from the power of death and as a potsherd shatter his enemy, amen! Encomium of Fiqetor
ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ እምስልጣነ ፣ ሞት ፣ ይሰውሮ ፣ ወከመ ፣ ገልፅ” ፣ ይቀጥቅጥ ፡ ፀሮ ፡፣ አሜን ። ። ንግባእኬ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ውዳሴሁ ፣ ለጥዑመ ፣ ዝክር ፡ ለፈጻሜ ፡ ስምዕ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ዘይቀንእ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ወክሐ ፣ ከሐዲያን ፡ ዘያጠፍእ ፣ ሥርናየ። ፣ ሀኬት ፣ ኢበልዐ ፣ በቅድመ ፡፣ ጉባኤ ፣ ተወደሰ” ፣ አፉሁ ፣ ከሠተ ፣ በሕግ ፡ ወበዐቅም” ፣ ሠርዐ” ፣ ለልሣኑ ። አባ ፣ አባ ፡ በመኑ ፣ አስተማስለከ ፣ እስጢፋኖስኑ ፣ ወልደ ፣ ፋሲሳደስ ፣ እብለከ ፡ እስመ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ኢፈርሀ ፡ ምረራ ፣ ለሞት ፡ ወአንተኒ ፣ አብደርከ ፣ ሞተ ፣ እምሕይወት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጽንዐ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፣ መጠውከ ፡፣ ነፍሰከ ፣፡ ወኢመሐከ ፣ ሥጋከ ፣ እፎ ፡ ዘኢያፍርሀከ ፡ ነበልባለ ፣ እሳት ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ስ ምፅከ ፡ ወጥዑም ፣ ዝክርከ ።
85 Let us return then to the praise (of him who is) of happy memory, of the perfecter of the testimony, zealous for the faith, a sturdy wrestler, who eliminates the tumult of the heretics, he did not eat the wheat of laziness, he was exalted before the assembly, he opened his mouth with discipline and disposed of his tongue in moderation. Abba! Abba! To whom must I compare you? Must I call you Stephen son of Basilides? Because he did not fear the bitterness of death, and you too preferred death to life (Jer 8:3); for the strength of the faith you offered your soul and did not spare your body, how is it that the flame of the fire did not frighten you? Good is your testimony and delicious is your memory.


[p. 226] {#po.51.p226}
ወርቅኑ ፡ እብለከ ፣ እስመ ፣ ለወርቅ ፣ በእሳት ፡ ያሜክርዎ ፣ ወምን ተ ፡ ይሬስዮ ፣ እሳት ፡ ለወርቅ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ዘያጸርዮ ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፣ ለዘ ፣ ቦቱ ፣ ትዕግስት"! ። ብሩርኑ ፡ እብለከ ፡ በከውር” ፣ ዘተነጥፈ ፡ በሕን ቅርተ ፡ ክብር ፡ ዘተፈትነ ፡ ዘአጽረይዎ ፡ ምስብዒተ ። አማን ፣ ብሩር ፣ አንተ ፣ ዘኢያፍርሀከ ፡ ግርማ ፡ ነድ ፣ በቅድመ ፣ ጉባኤ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ክቡድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታጽርዕ ፣ ዘሃይማኖት ፣ ካሕድ ፣ በትዕግሥትከ ፡ ንኡድ ። ብሩር ፣ ኢይሜንዮ ፡ ወወርቅ ፣ ኢይላሕዮ ፡ ለንጽሐ ፡ ሃይማኖትከ ፣ አባ ፡ አባ ፣ መኑ ፡ ይረክባ ፣ ለትዕግሥትከ ፣ ወበአይቴ ፡ ይትረከብ ፡ ዘከማከ ፣ በትዕግስትከ ፣ አጥረይካ ፡፣ ለነፍስከ ።
86 Must I perhaps call you gold, because they test gold with fire (Sir 2:5), and what could fire do to gold if not purify it, and so also affliction to him who has patience? Must I perhaps call you silver that has been filtered in the furnace, tested in the furnace of glory that they have purified seven times (Ps 11:7)? You are truly silver that the terror of the flame did not frighten before the assembly of a numerous people (Ps 34:18), so that you might remove the betrayal of the faith, praised for your patience. Silver is not more beautiful and gold is not better than the purity of your faith. Abba! Abba! Who can find your patience? And where can one find (someone) like you? With your patience you have acquired your soul (Lk 21:18). You have indeed become the plant of the testimony in Ethiopia and many have rejoiced in you.
እስመ ፣ ተክለ ፣ ስምዕ ፡፣ በኢትዮጵያ ፣ ኮንከ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፣ ተፈሥሑ ፡፣ ብከ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ሰሎሞን ፡ ጥበብሰ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይትለአክዋ ፡ እምሕማም ፡ ባልሐት ፣ ወአርአየት ፡፣ መንግሥተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ወወሀበቶ ፣ አእምሮ ፡፣ ቅዱሳን ፣ ወአብዐለቶ ፡ በጻማ ፣ ወአብዝኅት ፣ ጥሪቶ ፡ ወተማሕፀነቶ ፡ እም ፀር ፣ ወእምእለ” ፣ ይትዐገቱ ፡ ዐቀበቶ” ፣ ወበገድል ፡ ጽኑዕ ፣ አሞአቶ ። አማን ፣ ተልእካ ፣ ለጥበብ ፡ ወኮነቶ ፡ አበ ፣ ወእመ ፡ አኅለየቶ ፡ በትዕግ ሥት ፣ ወባልሐቶ ፣ እምሕማም ፡ አርአየቶ ፣ መንግሥተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ወአብዐለቶ ፣ በጻማ ። ተንተኑ ፣ እለ ፡ ተቃወምዎ ፣ እስመ ፡ ብሕሙ ፡ በሰ ይፈ ፣ ግዘት ፡ ውእቱሰ ፡ አጥረየ ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ስመ ፡ ዘኢየኀልቅ ፡ ዘይቴ ይስ ፡ እምውሉድ ፡ ወአዋልድ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በሃይማኖት ፡ ወረከባ ፡ ለም ንግሥተ ፣ ሰማያት ።
87 As Solomon says: 'Wisdom has delivered from suffering those who serve her' (Wis 10:9-10) and has manifested the kingdom of God, and has given him the knowledge of the saints and has enriched him with labor, has multiplied his possessions and has preserved him from the enemy, and has protected him from those who surrounded him and has allowed him to win with a vigorous battle. He has truly served wisdom and she has become father and mother to him (4Esd 13:55), she has corroborated him in patience, she has delivered him from suffering and has made him see the kingdom of Mount Sayon and has enriched him with labor. Those who opposed him have wavered because they have been silenced with the sword of excommunication, while he has acquired an eternal name that does not run out, that is worth more than sons and daughters; he became a martyr in the faith and has obtained the kingdom of heaven.
ናሁ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ በላዕሌሁ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡፣ ሰሎሞን ፣ ከመ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ዘበ ፡ ምንሃብ ፣ አመከሮሙ ፡ ወከመ ፣ ጽንሓሓ ፣ መስዋዕት ፡ ተወክፎሙ ፡ ወበመ ዋዕለ ፣ ዋሕዮሙ ፣ ይበርሁ ፡ ወከመ ፣ ቀለምጺጽ"' ፣ ውስተ ፡ ብርዕ ፡ ይረውጹ ። ወይኩንኑ ፣ አሕዛበ ፡ ወይእኅዙ ፣ ሕዝበ ፣ ወይነግሥ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ለዓለም ። አማንኬ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ አንተ ፡ ተመክሮ ፡ በአሳት ፡ ፈተውከ ፣ ቀነትከ ፡፣ ሐቁቄከ ፣ ወድኅሪተ ፡ ኢሐረስከ ፣ ጸመድከ ፣ አባዕሪከ ፡ ወኢያጽነንከ ፣ ትልመከ ። ዘራእከ ፣ ስርናየከ ፡ በጻማ ፡ ወበትፍሥሕት ፡፣ አረርከ ። አማን ፡ ወርቅ ፡ አንተ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እደ ፡ ኬንያ ፡፣ ዘኅረዮ ፡ ወልደ ፡፣ ማርያም ፣ ለርእሱ ፣ ዘአንደያ ፣ ለመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ዘአጥረያ ፣ ለላህበ ፣ እሳት ፣ ዘኢፈርሀ ፡ ዋዕያ ።
88 Behold, what Solomon said has been fulfilled in him: 'He has tested them like gold in the foundry and as a holocaust he has accepted them, in the days of their visitation they will shine and like sparks in the stubble they will run. They will govern peoples, they will dominate a people and God will reign over them forever' (Wis 3:6-8). You are truly gold; you have loved the test with fire, you have girded your loins (Eph 6:14) and have not plowed (looking) back (Lk 9:62), you have put the yoke on your oxen and have not distorted your furrow. You have sown your grain with labor and with joy you have reaped (Ps 125:6). You are truly gold, work of the hand of the artist, whom the Son of Mary has chosen, who has made himself poor (2Cor 8:9), who has acquired the kingdom of heaven, who has not feared the heat of the flame of the fire, to become an apostle of the preaching of Mount Sayon.
ለሰቢከ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ከዊነ ፣ ሐዋርያ ። ጥዩቀ ፣ ኮነ ፣ ዘይቤ ፡ መክብብ ፣ ጥበብ ፣ ከሠተት ፡ አፈ ፡ በሃም ፡ ወልሳናቲሆሙ ፡፣ ለሕፃናት ፡፣ ረሰየት ፣ ርቱዐ ። ሕፃን ፡ እብሎ ፡ በእንተ ፣ የውሃቱ ፣ እስመ ፣ አልቦ ፣ ዘይትዔረያ ፡ ለየዋሀቱ ። ጠቢብ ፣ እብሎ ፡ በእን ተ ፡ ትምህርቱ ፣ ወአእምሮቱ ፡፣ እስመ ፡ በጥበብ ፡ ወበምክር ፡ ይትናገር ፣ ወቦቱ ፡ ርኅራጌ ፡፣ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ” ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይሬኢ ፣ ርቱበ ። ወያስተረክብ ፣ ለጸሎት ፣ ወኢያሰትት ፣ ተመጥዎ ፣ ሥጋ ፡፣ ወደም ፡ እንዘ ፣ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብሩ ።
89 It has become certain, what Ecclesiastes says: 'Wisdom has opened the mouth of the mute and has rectified the speech of children' (Wis 10:21). I call him a child for his meekness, because there is no one who equals his meekness. I call him wise, for his erudition and for his knowledge, because he spoke with wisdom and with counsel and had compassion for all and especially when he saw a hungry person, and he was dedicated to prayer and did not neglect to receive the Body and the Blood when he was in his monastery.

### Coronation of Fiqetor
ወይቤ ፡፣ ርኢኩ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት! ፣ ፻ ፣ ግጻዌ ፣ ብሩሃነ” ፣ አን በረ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ዲበ ፣ ርእስየ ፡ ወካልኡ ፣ አጽንዐ ፡ ወሣልሱ ፡ አክበደ ። ነጽሩ ፣ ዘተውህበ ፡ ሎቱ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፣ በሕይወቱ ፣ በኅብረተ ፡ ሥላሴ ፣ ተሰርገወ ፡ አክሊለ ፣ ዘከመ ፡ በረድ ፡ ርእየቱ ። ወካዕበ ፣ ይቤ ፣ ሰተይኩ ፡ ጽዋዐ ፡ ማየ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ጻዕዳ ፡ ዘቦቱ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፣ አብ ፡፣ ለከልሎቱ ፡ ሠምረ ፡፣ ወልድ ፣ ለአስተቀጽሎቱ ። አሠነየ ፣ መንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ለአሰርግዎቱ ፡፣ ወአስተዮ ፣ ጽዋዐ ፣ ሕይወት ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይክሀል ፣ ጸላኢ ፣ አስግሮቶ ። ነቢየኑ” ፣ እብሎ ፣ ወሚመ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡፣ እስመ ፣ ከመ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ረአዬ ፣ ኅቡአት ፡ ውእቱ ። ነቢያትሰ ፣ ርእዩ ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ ለደኃሪ ፡ እምርኀቅ ፡ መዋዕል ። ወሐዋርያትኒ ፣ ሰበኩ ፡ ዘርእዩ ፣ ወዘሰምዑ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፣ አቡነ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፣ ተመሰሎሙ ፣ ለክልኤሆሙ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ለርእሱ ፣ ዘይደልዎ ፡ ወተ
90 And he said: 'One day I saw three shining Persons. One (of them) placed a crown on my head, the second confirmed (it) and the third made it heavy.' Observe what was offered to him while he was (still) alive; he was adorned with the communion of the Trinity with a crown that resembled snow. And again he said: 'I drank a cup that had water of life, white!' The Father rejoiced in crowning him, the Son was pleased in girding him, the Holy Spirit did (even) better in adorning him. And he made him drink the cup of life, so that the enemy would not trap him. Must I call him a prophet or an apostle? Because he is a seer of hidden things like the prophets. As for the prophets, they saw in advance what was to happen in the future. And the Apostles also preached what they had seen and what they had heard. And this father of ours, Fiqetor, also truly resembled both, he saw what would have benefited himself, and before he died he received the glory of the heavens.


[p. 228] {#po.51.p228}
ወፈየ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ፣ ዘእምቅድመ ፣ ይሙት ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ ኮነ ፣ ጽኑዐ ፡ ለተጋድሎ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ዘተውህቦ ፡ በኀበ ፡ ልዑል ፣ ወአርብሐ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግብር ። ከመ ፣ ነቢያት ፡ ርእየ ፣ ኅቡአተ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ዘሀሎ ። ወከመ ፣ ሐዋርያት ፡ ሰበከ ፡ ዘተገ ብረ ፡፣ ኩሎ ። ትእዛዘ ፣ ርእሱ ፣ አብጢሎ ፡፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ኮነ ፡ ክፍሎ ፣ ነቅዐ ፣ በረከት ፡ አጥለሎ ፡
91 And therefore he became strong in the battle so that he could receive what was given to him by the Most High and made his soul profit in every work. Like the Prophets he saw hidden things that were to happen. And like the Apostles he preached everything that had been done. Eliminating his will, he became part of the Lord (Ps 15:5), the spring of blessing watered him, the stream of the Scriptures enriched him.

### Fiqetor foretells the coming of the bishops to Ethiopia
ውሒዘ ፣ መጻሕፍት ፡፣ አብዐሎ ። ወእምዝ ፣ ሶበ ፡ አውሥኦ ፡፣ ንጉሥ ፣ መፍቀሬ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፣ ዘርአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፣ ተማህለል ፡ አንተ ፡፣ ወአኃዊከ ፡ በጽህቀተ ፣ ሕሊና ፣ ከመ ፣ የሀበነ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣
92 And after this Zar'a Ya'aqob, the king who loves God, spoke to him saying: 'Beseech, you and your brothers for the sake of the faith, with diligence of thought, so that God may grant our land an upright bishop!' And our father

Fiqetor spoke immediately and said: 'Regarding the bishops, they have (already) left and are along the road because they have hindered their path.' And the messiah of God said to him: '(May God) Do according to your word, because they are the light of the world.' And he had said this in the month of Tarr without any voice having been heard. And after ten months had (passed) after (Fiqetor) had spoken, the news of their arrival was heard and there was joy in the whole region. What (great) power have those who have been chosen in Christ manifested! And this father of ours, Fiqetor, saw hidden things, and proclaimed the great works (of God), and performed wonders because the medicine of the Holy Spirit was given to him. May the strength of his prayer be of help to us! Amen. Investiture of Fiqetor: second version
ኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ይኩነነ ፡ ረድኤተ ፣ አሜን ። ወአረጋዊሰ ፣ አባ ፡፣ ተወልደ ፣ መድኅን ፡ ያፈቅሮ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፣ እምኩ ሎሙ ፡ ውሉዱ ፡ እስመ ፡ የሐውር ፣ በሥምረተ ፡ ልቡ ። ወይከሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ጥበቦ ፣ ወምክሮ ፣ ግብሮ" ፣ ወግብረ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ወአልቦ ፣ ዘየኀብኦ ፡ በኩሉ ፣ ፍናዊሁ ፡፣ እስመ ፡፣ ኮነ ፡ ለባዌ ፣ ትምህርቱ ፡ ወተላዌ ፡ ፍኖቱ ። ወያፈቅሮ ፣ በኩሉ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ ወበኩሉ ፡፣ ነፍሱ ። ወእ ምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ደክመ ፡፣ አረጋዊ ፡ ወረስዐ ፡ በሕቁ ፣ እስመ ፡ ኮነ ፣ መዋዕ ሊሁ ፡ ፻ቋወ፻ ፡፣ ወእምዝ ፡፣ አንበረ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ቅዱስ ፣ ፊቅጦር ፡ ወባረኮ ፣ በረከተ ፡፣ መንፈሳዌ ። ወነበረ ፡ በምድረ ፣ ሠራዌ ፡ 'ብፁዕ ፡ ፊቅጦር” ፣ እንተ ፣ ትሰመይ ፡ ማይ ፡፣ ሳግላ ፡ ቀትረ ፡ ይውዕል ፡ ውስተ ፣ ጸድፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ በላዕሌሃ ፡ ይትሐነጽ ፣ ውሣጤ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወሌሊተ ፣ ይመጽእ ፣ ቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ፡ በስማ ፣ ለእግዚእትነ ፣ አእሚሮ ፡ በመንፈስ"! ።
93 And the old man Abba Tawalda-Madahn loved him more than all his children because he walked according to the will of his heart. And to him he revealed his wisdom and his counsel, his work and the work of his fathers, and in all his ways there was nothing that he hid from him, because he had become one who assimilated his instruction and who followed his way. And he loved him with all his heart and with all his thought and with all his soul (Dt 6:5; Mt 22:37). And after that, when the old man grew weary and became very old, for his days were one hundred and thirty-three, and after this he imposed his hands on the holy Fiqetor and blessed him with a spiritual blessing. And he placed him on his chair so that he might become the father of the saints. And the blessed Fiqetor lived in the land of Sarawe which is called May Sagla; by day he stayed in the precipice to seek water inside it and returned at night to the church that bore the name of our Lady, because he had known in the Spirit

### Death of Abba Tawalda-Madahn
ወአዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፣ ተወልደ ፣ መድኅን ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ ወቋ ለወርኀ ፡ የካ ቲት ፣ በሰላመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ በረከቱ ፡ ትኩን ፣ ምስሌነ ፣ አሜን ። ወብ ፁዕሰ ፡ አባ ፡ ፊቂጦር ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ካዕበተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ እምአቡሁ ፣ አረጋዊ ፡ ከመ ፣ ነሥአ ፣ ኤልሳፅ ፡ ካዕበተ ፣ መንፈሱ ፡፣ ለኤልያስ ፡ ነቢይ ።
94 And Abba Tawalda-Madahn rested on the eighteenth of the month of Yakkatit in the peace of God, may his blessing be with us! Amen. And as for the blessed Abba Fiqetor, he received double the spirit from his old father, as Elisha had received double the spirit of the prophet Elijah (2Kgs 2:9).

### Abba Fiqetor takes the reins of the community

95 And he gathered all those who were at Dabra Maryam, the great and the small, and added to them a rigorous regulation. He bound them with obedience and admonished them according to the law, and made the works of penance onerous for them, and arranged for them (one) bread and (one) chalice so that they would not eat alone except in the community. And he set a limit so that they would not transgress his word and so that they would be more solicitous for the place of instruction and for the listening to the word of God. And he himself became an example for them and the brothers rejoiced at the beauty of his teaching. Indeed he became capable in his word and in his work. 96 And he was given great authority and his word was sharpened like a sword (Heb 4:12) and the great of the land feared for his word and obeyed his word and there was no one who transgressed his word. And he made the wisdom of this world very foolish (1Cor 1:20). And the testimony of his word went out everywhere. And the chiefs of the region and all the great of the country came to ask him for forgiveness and learned from him the word of God. He made sinners return and delivered the afflicted and gladdened the sad. And he interceded for the prisoners before the kings and the princes and saved them from their disgrace. As Job says thus: 'As for me, I weep for every afflicted person and I shed tears if I see a suffering person, I have held fast to what is good' (Job 30:25)
ወከማሁ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ያነብፅ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ነፍሰ ፣ ርኅብተ ፣ ወኢይፈርህ” ፣ ምንዳቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተዐርቅ ፡ ጽልአ ። ወያድኅኖ ፡ ለነዳይ ፣ እምእደ ፣ ዘይትዔገሎ ። እስመ ፡ ዴገና ፣ ለሠናይት ፡ ወኀደጋ ፡ ለእኪት ፣ ወተወደሰ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግብሩ ፡ ዘነበበ ፡፣ ይከውኖ ። በከመ ፣ ይቤ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፣ ብሩር ፡ ርሱን ፡፣ ከናፍረ ፡ ጻድቃን ። እስመ ፡ ተውህቦ ፡፣ ሥልጣነ” ፣ መባ ሕት ፣ እምኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትናገር ፡ ርትዐ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ነገሥት ፣ ወመኳንንት ፡ ወበመድበለ ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ወኢያደሉ ፡ ለባዕል ፡ ወለነ ዳይ ፣ ወአልቦቱ ፣ ነሚአ ፣ ገጽ ፡ እስመ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይገ ብር ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ፀሐይ ፣ ትምህርቱ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣
97 So also our father Fiqetor shed tears when he saw a hungry soul and did not fear affliction to reconcile hatred. And he saved the poor from the hand of those who wanted to rob him. Indeed he pursued the good and abandoned the evil and was praised for every work of his; what he said came true. As Solomon says: 'The lips of the righteous are fired silver' (Prov 10:20). Because he had been given great authority from God so that before the kings and the princes and the assembly of the community he might say what is right and not make preferences between the rich and the poor, and there was no partiality with him (1Pt 1:17) because he acted (only) for the Lord. And for this reason his teaching shone like the sun. As says

ጳውሎስ ፡ ወታስተርእዩ ፡ ከመ ፣ ብርሃናት ፣ ውስተ ፣ ዓለም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትሜ ህሩ ፡ ቃለ ፣ ሕይወት ። ከመዝ ፣ ነበረ ፣ እንዘ ፡ ይሜህር" ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ማኅ በረ ፣ ደብረ ፡ ማርያም ።
Paul: 'And you must appear as lights in the world while you teach the word of life' (Phil 2:15). It was so when he taught the assembly of the community of Dabra Maryam.

### Zar'a Ya'aqob becomes a member of the Eustathians
ወብፁዕሰ ፣ ዘርአ ፣ ያዕቆብ ፣ ማኅበሮሙ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ለአባ ፡ ኤውስጣቴ ዎስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ አብሳዲ ፡ ወደቂቆሙ ፡ ወተካየደ ፡ ኪዳነ ፣ ፍቅር ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ምስለ ፣ አባ ፣ ተወልደ ፣ መድኅን ፡፣ በመንፈስ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ ስርግው ። ወዝን ቱሰ ፣ አቡነ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፣ ወራሴ ፣ መንበሮሙ ፣ ያፈቅሮ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ ለመ ሲሕ ፣ ወይትመሀለል ፣ በእንቲአሁ ፣ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርታዕ ፣ መንግ ሥቱ ፣ ወይጥፍኡ ፡፣ ዐላዊያን ፡ በእብሬቱ ።
98 And the blessed Zar'a Ya'aqob became a community of Abba Ewostatewos and of Abba Absadi and of their children and established an eternal covenant of love with Abba Tawalda-Madehn, adorned with the Holy Spirit. And this father of ours, Fiqetor, truly heir to their seat, loved the anointed one very much and interceded for him every day so that his kingdom might function rightly and so that during his era the rebels might be destroyed.

### Visions of Fiqetor
ወይቤ ፣ ፣ ብእሲ ፣ ሃይማኖታዊ ፡ ሖርኩ ፣ ኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ እትኀ ሠሥ ፣ እምኔሁ ፣ ኅቡአቲሁ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ተፈሣሕ ፡ አንተ ፣ ወኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፣ ያፈቅርዎ ፣ ለንጉሥ ። ሰይፈ ፡ ሥላሴ ፣ ተመልሐ ፡፣ ከመ ፡ ይም ትር ፡ ፀሮ ፣ እምድር ። ሰማዕኩ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘይብል ፡፣ ይንግሥ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ በኩለሄ” ፣ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዘርአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ በዐደል ፡ ወበዳለክ ፡ ወከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ሥልሰ ።
99 And a religious man said: 'I went to him to ask him about his hidden things. And he said to me: "Rejoice, you and all those who love the king. The sword of the Trinity has been unsheathed so that it may cut off his enemy from the earth. I have heard the word that says: 'May the Trinity reign everywhere during the days of Zar'a Ya'aqob, in the Adal and in the Dalak'"'—so he said three times.

100 'And after this, I saw while he was piercing with his hand the lost Arwe Badlay. Is there perhaps someone (else) who has obtained such a vision other than this blessed one, who has observed the commandment of God? Truly! Truly! The infidel was punished with the sword of the Trinity, his corpse was abandoned on the face of the earth and became food for the birds (1Sam 17:46), the power of his prayer was praised and behold it has been manifested. Arwe was trampled and became dust, the king rejoiced and was very glad because he had brought down devastation upon his enemy.'

### Fiqetor restores sight to a blind man

him hear this and you will find the cure by his hand." And guiding him they made him arrive at Abba Fiqetor, and he said this before the whole assembly. And this father of ours, warmed by the Holy Spirit, gave order to the assembly so that they might prostrate themselves three times and say: "Christ Lord, have mercy on us!" After that, he raised his hand, spat his saliva and made the sign in the name of the Trinity and anointed his eyes and (the blind man) was healed immediately. And all those who had seen his prayer praised, because God had made him great.' We cannot exalt the blessed man whom God has praised; blessed are his feet that did not go to what is vain! Blessed are his limbs that were not tainted by the filth of sin! Blessed are his lips that made the word of divinity flow! And he made all the days of his life pass teaching in peace. Fiqetor's farewell to his own and his death
ወሶበ ፣ ቀርበ ፡ ፍልሰቱ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡፣ ኮነ ፡ ይምዕዶሙ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ወንጌል ፣ ጥዑም ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አበዊሁ ። ወይኬ ፣ ለአባግዒሁ ፡ በኀጢአ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢይትረከብ ፡ ዘከማሁ ። እፎ ፣ ኮኑ ፣ ይእተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ አይኑ ፡ ምድር ፡ ጾረቶሙ ፡ ወአይኑ ፡ መካን ፣ አግመሮሙ ። ወሊተሰ ፣ ይመስለኒ ፡ ደመና ፣ ሐዘን ፣ ዘዐገቶሙ ፡ በዐውሎ ፡፣ ዕንባዜ ፣ ዘከደኖሙ ፣ እስመ ፣ ድክትምና ፣ ደደቀቶሙ ፡ በዘ ፡ ኀጥኡ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፡ ምንሐረ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወብሉይ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲፻ ፡ወ፳ ፡ ለኅዳር ፡ በሰላመ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በረከቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ስምዑ ፣ አበዊየ ፣ ወአኃዊየ ፡ ዘገብረ ፣ እምድኅረ” ፣ ሞቱ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አስተርአየኒ ፣ በሌሊት ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ዘኢጸሐፍከ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ አነ ፡ ቀናኢ ፣ ለሃይማኖት ። አኮኑ ፡፣ ነበርኩ ፡ አነ ፣ እንዘ ፡ አሐውር ፡ ለኀግሂሠ ፣ ሕግ ፣ ወዖድኩ"” ፣ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ደወለ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ወብ ዙኀ ፡ ተመንደብኩ ፡ በፍኖት ፣ ወኢፈራህኩ ፣ ሐሩረ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ኅድግሰ ፡ ሊተ ፣ ለዘ ፣ አልበሰ ፣ አሐደ ፡ ልብሰ ፣ ለነዳይ ፣ ይዌድስዎ ፡ አንሰ ፣ ጻመ ውኩ ፣ በእንተ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ አርትዕ ፡ ቃለ ፡፣ ሃይማኖት ።
102 And when his passing from this world drew near, he exhorted them with the good word of the Gospel. And after that, he rested and was buried with his fathers. Woe to his little sheep, for being deprived of him, because there is not found (anyone) like him. How were they that day (his children)? What earth bore them? And what place contained them? And it seems to me that a cloud of mourning blocked them, that the whirlwind of discouragement struck them. Indeed, the condition of being an orphan befell them because they lost their father, the channel of the New and Old (Testament). And his rest occurred on the fifteenth of Hedar in the peace of God; may his blessing be with us! Amen. Fiqator appears after his death 103 Listen, my fathers and my brothers, to what he did after his death. A certain man said: "He appeared to me in the night and said to me: 'Why have you not written of me that I am zealous for the faith? Have I not lived walking in the search for the law, and have I not traveled throughout the territory of Ethiopia, and have I not found myself so much in danger along the road, and have I not feared the scorching of the sun? (If) they praise him who gave a garment to the needy, [how much more me, who labored for God so that I could rectify the word of the faith?']"


[p. 238] {#po.51.p238}

### Eulogy of Fiqator

104 Abba! Abba! To whom shall I compare you? To Noah the righteous (Sir 44:17), or to Moses the meek (Sir 45:4), to Daniel who discerns, to Ezra the father of the scriptures, to David filled with sense, and to Solomon the wise? To whom, like whom shall I compare you? Perhaps to Daniel, the seer of hidden things, or to Ananias and Azariah and Mishael, those who quenched the furnace of Babylon (Dn 3:24ff)? Daniel said: "I saw there a man who was clothed in linen and girded at his loins with gold of Uphaz, and his body also was like beryl, and his face like the appearance of lightning, and his eyes like lamps of fire, and his arms and his feet like the gleam of burnished bronze, and the sound of his words like the voice of many people" (Dn 10:5-6). And you too in this (world) have seen, caught up in the spirit, a tremendous vision, the Lord of lights who is all light.
አንበረ ፣ አብ ፣ አክሊለ ፣ ጸጋ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርእስከ” ፡ ወበወልዱ ፡ አሠነየ ፣ ለከ ፣ ወበመንፈሱ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ አጽንዐከ ። ኦኅሩይ ፡ ወብፁዕ ፡ ፊቅጦር ፣ ሐዳሴ ፣ ስምዕ ፡ አንተ ፡፣ ውእቱ ፡ ዘተመሰልከ ፡ ከመ ፡ አናንያ ፡ ወአዛርያ ፣ ወሚሳኤል ፡ እለ ፣ በጥብዓቶሙ ፡ አጐረሩ ፡ እሳተ ፡ እቶኖሙ ፡ ለፋርስ ፣ ወአግሀዱ ፣ አምልኮ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፣ በቅድመ ፡፣ ናቡከድናጾር ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወአንተ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በዝየ ፡ አቱረርከ ፣ መዓተ ፣ ካሕዶሙ ፡ ለኢት ዮጵያ ፣ እለ ፣ ተዋክሑ ፡ በእንተ ፣ መንግሥተ ፡፣ ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ። ወአግሀ ድከ ፣ ተስፋ ፣ ቅዱሳን ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ይኩን ፣ ጽኑሐ” ፡ በቅድሜሁ ፣ ለመፍ ቀሬ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘርአ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ኦብፁዕ ፡ ወኅሩይ ፡ በኢሳይያስኑ” ፡ ወልደ ፡ አሞጽ ፡ አስተማስለከ ፡ ወሚመ ፡ በሱቱኤል” ፣ ዕዝራ ፡ ዘሰትየ ፡ ጽዋዐ ፣ ልቡና ፣ እስመ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ አንተሂ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ መሰጡከ” ፣ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎከ ፣ በሕይወትከ ፡ ወአ ብኡከ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፣ ዘበሰማያት ፣ ወተጽሕፈ ፡ ስምከ ፡ በቀለመ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ምስለ ፣ ሐዋርያት ። ኢሳይያስ ፣ ይቤ ፣ ርኢክዎ ፡ ለፍቁር ፣ በናዝሬት ፡ ሀሎ ፣ ይጠቡ ፡ ከመ ፣ ሕፃን ። ወበከመ ፣ ሥርዓት ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ከመ ፣ ኢይት አመር ። ወአንተ ፡ ትቤ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ሀሎኩ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጸሎት ፣ ርኢኩ ፡ ሕፃነ” ፣ ዘሠኖይ ፣ ራእዩ ፣ ወአዕይንቲሁ ፣ ይመስል ፡ ከመ ፣
105 The Father placed upon your head the crown of grace, and through his Son, for you, he did something even better, and by his Holy Spirit he strengthened you. O elect and blessed Eiyathiyog, renewer of the testimony! You are he who resembled Ananias, Azariah, and Mishael, who by their courage cooled the fire of the furnace of Persia and revealed the worship of God before King Nebuchadnezzar. And you, again, have here cooled the wrath of the heresy of Ethiopia, those who raised contentions regarding the kingdom of Mount Sayon. And you have unveiled the hope of the saints that awaits its own realization, in the presence of King Zär'a Ya'aqob, the lover of God. 106 O blessed and elect, shall I compare you then to Isaiah son of Amoz (Is 1:1)? Or to Ezra Sutael (4 Esd 1:1) who drank the cup of understanding (4 Esd 14:41)? Indeed, you are also like him. You were caught up (in ecstasy) like Isaiah (Is 6), while still in (this) life of yours, and they introduced you into the Jerusalem that is in the heavens, and your name was written with golden ink together with the apostles. Isaiah said: "I saw the beloved in Nazareth: he was sucking (the breasts) like a child. And it is according to the disposition, so that it may not be known" (Ascls 11:17). And you said: "While I was in church at the time of prayer, I saw a child of beautiful appearance, and his eyes resembled the appearance of a shining star, and he rejoiced at


[p. 240] {#po.51.p240}
ሥርቀተ ፡ ቤዝ ፡፣ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ በቅድሜየ ፣ ወአነሂ' ፡ ተፈሣሕኩ ፡ በእን ቲአሁ ። ዕዝራ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በህየ ፡ ሰትየ ፡ ጽውዐ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ዘኅብሩ ፡ ይመ ስል ፣ እሳተ ፡ ወከብደ ፡ ልቡና ፡ ውስተ ፣ እንግድዓሁዎሞ ፣ ወነበበ ፣ 2ወ፪ ለእግዚአብሔር ፣፡ መጻሕፍቲሁ ።
my presence, and I too rejoiced because of him." The prophet Ezra in that age drank an entire cup whose color resembled fire, and intelligence grew heavy in his breast, and he uttered ninety-four books of God (4 Esd 14:40-45).
ወአንተሂ ፣ በዝየ ፣ በኀይለ ፡ መንፈስ ፣ ከዊነከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ጽዋዐ ፡ ልቡና ፡ ሰተይከ ፡ ዘይጸዐዱ ፣ ፈድፋደ ፣ እምሐሊብ ፣ ወይጥዕም ፡ 'እም ፣ መዓር ፣ ወሶከር ። ወከብደ ፡ ዲበ ፣ እንግድዓከ ፡ ልቡና ፡ ወፈከ ርኮን ፣ ለቃለ መጻሕፍት ፡ በጥይቅና ። ኦብፁዕ ፡ ወኅሩይ ፡ አቡየ ፡ በመኑ” ፡ ከመ ፣ መኑ ፣ አስተማስለከ ። አንተኑ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ቀናኢ ፡ ለጽድቅ ፣ ወሚመ ፡፣ ሰባኬ ፣ ፍኖተ ፡ ንስሐ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ መጥምቅ ። አንተኑ ፣ ጴጥሮስ ፡፣ ኬፋ ፣ መሠረተ ፣ ከኒሳ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ጽኑዕ ። ወሚመ ፡ መምህረ ፣ አሕዛብ ፡ ልሳነ ፡ ፅፍረት ፣ ጳውሎስ ፣ ረድእ ፣ አንተኑ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ ፍቁረ ፡ እግዚእ ፣ ወሚመ ፡ እንድርያስ ፣ ሠናየ ፡፣ ርሥእ” ። አው ፣ ፊልሞና ፣ ሙሉአ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ዘአስ ተናገረ ፣ አዕዋፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰብእ ።
107 And now, you too, standing in heaven in the power of the spirit, have drunk the cup of intelligence, much whiter than milk and more delicious than the honeycomb and honey (Ps 118:103). And intelligence grew heavy upon your breast, and you explained with certainty the word of the Scriptures. O blessed and elect father of mine, with whom and to whom must I compare you? Are you perhaps Elijah, the zealous for justice, or John the Baptist, the preacher of the way of penance (Mt 3:1-12)? Are you perhaps Peter, Cephas, foundation of the congregation, solid rock (Mt 16:18)? Or Paul, the teacher of the Gentiles, fragrant speech? Are you the disciple John, the beloved of the Lord (Jn 13:23)? Or Andrew of the good old age? Or Philemon, filled with wisdom, who made the birds speak as (if they were) people?

108 Are you perhaps James son of Zebedee, filled with spirit, or the holy Thaddeus, teacher of Syria? Or the investiture of Judas, which the blessed Matthias took (Acts 1:25-26)? Are you the preacher of the Gospel, the joyful Matthew, or Nathanael, the pure Israelite, without guile (Jn 1:47); or the vigilant son of Alphaeus? Are you perhaps Bartholomew, who broke the bond of error with the word of faith; or the luminous Thomas, teacher of the Handake? Or Mark, preacher of the Gospel of the kingdom to those who were in Egypt? Are you perhaps Philip, who watered Samaria with the spring of four rivers (Acts 8:5), or James the Just, companion of the apostles? Or the disciple Clement, the lawgiver?

109 Are you perhaps Gregory, the worker of miracles and wonders? Or Basil, of sweet tongue and word? Or John Chrysostom, who (are) a wonder? Are you perhaps Peter, the perfection of the martyrs, a stable lamp for the church? Or Abba Benjamin, writer in the spirit? Or the archbishop Severus, the interpreter? Or, are you perhaps Ephrem, he who discerns, of sweet compositions? Or Cyril, of speech sharp as a metal sword? Or Abba Athanasius, image of the apostles, faithful man? Are you perhaps the second Anthony? Or Macarius, he who turned away from the voluptuousness of the world? Or Abba Shenoute, who contemplated hidden things? 110 We have compared your life to these and to those like these. Because you beat the footprints of those saints in love and performed their works. And you understood their words. O blessed and holy Fiqator, renewer of the testimony, according to valiant Angitos. May your word be for us power and firmness, amen. 111 Observe then, O my beloved, the goodness of this blessed one, since he loved his God with an intrepid heart. And he (God) made him a dwelling of the


[p. 244] {#po.51.p244}
ወነባቤ ፣ ቃለ ፣ መለኮት ። ብፁዕ ፡ ዘኀረዮ ፣ ብፁዕ ፡ ዘሰምሮ ፡ ብፁዕ ፣ ዘአፍቀሮ” ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኦአኃዊየ ፣ ንዑ ፡ ንብክዮ ፡ ወናስቆቅዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተኀኅጥአ ፣ ባሕርይ ፣ ዘኢይትረከብ ፣ በጥ ፡፣ ወከመ ፣ አድማስ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ በማእከለ ፣ ሀገር ።
Holy Spirit, and a speaker of the word of divinity. Blessed is he who chose him, blessed is he who was pleased (in him), blessed is he who loved him! Therefore, my brothers, come and let us weep for him and lament him, because the pearl that cannot be obtained at (any) price has been missing (Mt 13:45-46), and like a strong gem in the midst of the city (Am 7:8).
እበኪ ፣ ለርእስየ ፣ እንዘ ፣ እብል ፣ መኑ ፡ ይተረጉም ፡ ለነ ፣ ዘከ ማሁ ፣ እስመ ፣ ሙሐዘ ፡ ስብሐት ፣ አፉሁ ። አሌ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አሌ ፡ሊተ ፡ እስመ ፣ ረከበተኒ ፡ ሐዘን ፡ ወደደቀተኒ ፡ ድክትምና ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሄኖክ ፡ መኑ ፣ ይሁበኒ ፡ ለአፅይንትየ ፡ ከመ ፣ ይኩና ፡ ደመና ፣ ማይ ፡ ወእብኪ ፡ ዲቤክሙ ፣ ..............................................1ዊ፻1...]” እስመ ፣ ተፈልጠ ፣ እም ኔየ ፣ በእደ ፡ ሞት ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ርስቱ ፣ ዘለዓለም ።
112 I weep for myself, saying: "Who will interpret for us, as he (did)? A container of praise was indeed his mouth!". Woe to me! Woe to me! Mourning has indeed seized me, and the condition of being an orphan has befallen me. As Enoch says: "Who would give me that my eyes might become a cloud of water and I could weep over you" (En 95:1) [lacuna of five lines] because he has separated from me, by the hand of hard death, and has gone to his eternal inheritance.

### Epilogue

113 Behold, the proclamation of his goodness, of our father Fiqator, is finished. May his prayer and his blessing remain with our king Zär'a Ya'aqob and with all the Christian people, for ages of ages, amen! And for us who make his memory, may he write in the [lacuna of four lines] the sound of harps (Rev 14:2), where the river of the water of life flows from the throne (of the God) of hosts, where the tree of life produces fruit for every month, for twelve times (Rev 22:1-2), where the name of the twelve apostles is written (Rev 21:14): may he introduce us into it, without hindrance. And may he make us hear the voice of joy and gladness, when it shall descend from heaven prepared, the bride of the Lamb (Rev 21:2). When he shall come [lacuna of six lines] to hear his words, may God forgive us in common in the kingdom of heaven, amen. And do not say to me, Gäbrä Krastos, who had it written trusting in the prayer of my father Fiqator, that I am a liar, because God speaks through the mouth of all. And remember the donkey of Balaam (Nm 22:22ff), because it was a donkey but (7), our father ...
